Part 0

LixardUI configuration audit (branch config-simplify, 0.0.854)

Read-only. Nothing in the repo was changed. Full per-row inventories follow this summary, one part per module group. Every claim has a file:line reference.

Size of the settings today

Page Rows Advanced
Unit frames ~591 1
Party and raid ~493 0
Action bars 139 11
Tracker 68 6
Cooldowns 64 0
QoL (incl. 19 rows moved in from Bags/Tracker/Friends) 63 0
Minimap 59 6
Bags 53 3
Info bars 49 0
Chat (pilot) 41 4
Auras 21-25 0
Friends, Meters, Maps, Tooltips, Vendor, Mail, Auction see part 5
Core pages (General, Modules, Skins, Profiles, Wizard) see part 6

About 1,700 rows in all. 328 of the unit and group rows are the same 8-row text-style block repeated per text. About 650 rows have no description.

How the window works today

  • Core pages use O:AddPage; module pages use ns:AddOptionsPage. Look settings registered with ns:AddLookSpecs are routed back to their module by the HOMES table (General.lua:271-311). There are 8 hard-coded chapters (Options.lua:37-88).
  • tab = is set on 117 specs, but ShowTabs is only ever called with nil, so no tabs appear (Options.lua:737, 842). Tab names just get added to card titles.
  • "Show advanced" is gone; showAdvanced is dead. Advanced rows sit in a folded "Advanced" card. "Show help text" (global.showHelp) shows descriptions inline or as tooltips.
  • Search matches label, description, page, section, sub, tab and dropdown texts. It returns up to 150 rows, and clicking one jumps to the row and flashes it. There's no "/" focus.
  • Saving: Strip at logout keeps only non-default values on disk. Specs have only get/set closures, with no key path or default. So a "differs from default" marker or a reset needs a path field added to every spec (no SavedVariables change).
  • Reset traps: setting a key to nil makes reads fall through to the shared defaults table (later writes land in the defaults), and color arrays are stripped one element at a time.

Move mode today

  • /lui move: about 37 registration sites plus 14 Blizzard movers. Positions are stored only in profile.layouts.<layout>.<moverKey>, with snap, grid, attach and nudge. Layout itself stores no size or scale.
  • The gear dialog per mover picks rows from the module's options page by label text or position. Some have already broken this way: the Minimap border rows (Frame.lua:200) and the Action Bars / Cooldowns / Data Bars / Auras filters.
  • Sizes are stored in each module's own settings. Most appear on both the page and in the gear dialog; some only on the page, some only in the gear (micro/bag scale, eye scale, leave-vehicle size, chat width/height).
  • Positions stored outside the Layout system: bag and bank windows (bags.point, bags.bankPoint), Friends (global), Vendor, Mail and Auction (own keys in three different shapes), world map and Blizzard windows (qol.positions, qol.scales).
  • Size sliders that also move the frame: party/raid width/height and tracker Max height call Layout:Move (Headers.lua:317-320, Window.lua:1097-1103).
  • Cancel restores positions only, not sizes. Edit Mode has no Cancel and no Esc to leave. Debug logging (layoutLog, layoutProbe) still writes to SavedVariables.

Problem groups

  1. Look settings repeated per module. Corners appear in 9 modules, the border pair in 14, bar texture in 3+, plus outline and font in Chat, Tracker and Minimap. General's "Corners everywhere" and "Border everywhere" overwrite the module copies (Theme.lua:338-375). General's frame-text style only reaches Unit and Group frames; the others hard-code "OUTLINE". There are two accents: the user's "eye" amber and a fixed green.
  2. The same key or effect from two places. - Unit/group text: "Show name" vs the text style "Show". - Text size: 3-4 sliders. - Auto-sell: bags.autoSell vs vendor.autoSell.enabled. - Hiding the tracker in keys: keyOnly vs rules.challenge. - Talking head: QoL vs the Blizzard mover's "Hide it". - Save whispers: three spots. - Meters lock and numbers: page, gear and mover. - Vendor view: page and in-window menu. - showGrid: global and per bar. - Cooldowns icon size vs width.
  3. Inconsistent widgets and wording. - Switch vs dropdown for turning things off. - Opacity is 0-1 in one place, 40-100 in another and on/off in a third; sizes mix pixels, multiplier and %. - Paired names that mean the same thing: "Corner style" / "Corners", "Grow" / "Buttons go", "Time left" / "Show time left", "Item shape" = corners. - There's no segmented control or keybind widget in the builder.
  4. Dead or missing keys. - Never read: minimap.buttons.difficulty, minimap.buttons.queue, maps.scale, unitframes.resources, heals, the 7 shield/tint keys, showAdvanced. - Used but no default: tracker.barTexture, bankView, bankSortMode, bankSortBy, minimap.rotate, qol.cursorCustom, leaveSize, matchUndo. - Dead QoL sub-pages: "Selling and repair", "Mail", "Alerts".
  5. Bugs found on the way. Not fixed; they're listed so you can decide. - The 6 party/raid role-icon switches can't be reached (load order, GF Options.lua:627-629). - The Minimap gear drops its 2 border rows. - The tracker gear can lose its Background row (Dialog.lua:105-106). - The chat resize grip ignores the lock (Mover.lua:91-94). - Per-text Size shows 12 but draws 10 for power text. - The raid gear sticks to the first raid size opened. - The battle-res gear is empty on Forever. - Mythic+ timer texture lands on General > Bars. - Several setters skip LIXARDUI_SETTINGS_CHANGED.
  6. Combat items to check (from the regression pass): - "Hide it" on a core mover calls frame:Hide() with no combat check (Blizzard.lua:125-129). - Minimap button and drawer relayouts have no guard. - The queue eye anchor has no guard (Eye.lua:10-23). - Vendor calls C_MerchantFrame.GetItemInfo / IsMerchantItemRefundable with no Forever check.

What your target needs, and what blocks it

Target Status today What it takes Needs your decision?
4-6 common rows + "More options (n)" Advanced fold exists Mark 4-6 common specs per tab; the rest go into a counted fold. Pure UI. No, but I'll propose the common picks per tab for you to approve
Real tabs per page tab set on 117 specs, never shown Turn on the existing ShowTabs path No
Shared look + Override / Use shared Global values exist; modules keep their own copies with their own defaults "Inherit" must be a state. Either an absent module value means inherit (possible only where the module default already equals the shared default), or a new inherit flag key. Modules whose default differs from the shared one (e.g. chat outline) would look different if they inherited. Yes
Global search with "/" focus, real control in results Search exists, no "/" Add the "/" key while the window has focus; result rows already build the real control No
One widget set + one-line desc everywhere No segmented or keybind widget; ~650 rows without desc Add a segmented widget; write the ~650 descriptions No
Changed-from-default marker + Reset tab Specs don't know their key or default Add path to each spec; resolve defaults from ns.defaults; reset by writing the default copy, never nil (trap above) No
Position/size stored once, move mode = Position tab Mostly one store, but 6 window families use their own keys Pointing them at profile.layouts would be a migration Yes, you asked me to stop here
Move-mode popover (position, scale, anchor) + "All settings" The gear holds look and behavior rows Move gear-only rows onto their module page, then reduce the gear to position/scale/anchor Yes: which gear-only rows move where (list in part 6 B2/B3)

Part 1

Settings audit: Chat (pilot), Friends, Meters, Maps, Tooltips, Vendor, Mail, Auction

Repo: LixardUI (read-only audit, 2026-11-10, version 0.0.854).

How the rows reach the page (read this first)

  • A module's Options.lua calls ns:AddOptionsPage(page) (LixardUI/Core/Entry.lua:25-32). This drops retailOnly sections and specs when not on retail, and foreverOnly ones when not on Forever.
  • ns:AddLookSpecs(title, specs, sub) (Entry.lua:11-13) queues look rows. When the Options addon loads, Pages/General.lua:295-320 (MoveHome plus the HOMES table at General.lua:270-293) moves them back onto the module's own page. Each lands in a new section named after its kind ("Fonts", "Borders and corners", "Bars", "Backgrounds", "Look") inside a sub-page that differs per module:
  • Chat goes to Window.
  • Friends goes to Friends.
  • Meters goes to Layout.
  • Vendor goes to View.
  • Mail and Auction go to General.
  • Tooltips has no sub, so every kind becomes its own sidebar sub-page.
  • Pages/Extra.lua:31-48 then moves sections that carry home= to another page:
  • Chat's "Whispers" section moves to Friends > Whispers.
  • Friends' "Keystones" section moves to QoL > Mythic+.
  • Bags' "Selling and protection" section moves to Vendor > Repair and sell > Bags tab.
  • Two QoL sections move to Auction > Crafting orders.
  • Pages/Extra.lua:19-29 drops specs whose skipIf() returns true:
  • Friends' copy of the whisper settings is dropped when Chat is loaded.
  • Bags' "Sell junk" switch is dropped when Vendor is loaded.
  • Sub-page building (LixardUI_Options/Options.lua:122-157): if any section has a sub, sections without one go into a sub-page called "General". If no section has a sub, each section title becomes its own sub-page.
  • Advanced rows (Options.lua:595-636): each sub-page collects its advanced=true rows into one extra "Advanced" card at the bottom. The card shows only when global.showAdvanced is on (Options.lua:33, 190).
  • Mover popups (LixardUI/Layout/Dialog.lua:102-117) build extra compact rows from options.settings(). They are listed separately for each module below.

Notation: - adv = advanced=true. - SV paths start at ns.DB (profile / global / char). - L.D = profile.layouts["Default"]. Layout.lua:13 sets SHARED_LAYOUT = "Default", and Layout.lua:24-38 falls back to the biggest saved layout. - The counts assume retail, with Chat and Friends both loaded.

1. Chat (LixardUI_Chat), page key chat

TOC load order (LixardUI_Chat.toc): Chat, Copy, Links, Buttons, Mover, History, Format, Log, Filters, WhisperHistory, WhisperTab, OptionsMore, Options, Init.

Where the defaults live: - profile.chat: Chat.lua:9-28 - profile.chatFilters: Filters.lua:8-17 - profile.chatFormat: Format.lua:7-13 - profile.chatLog and char.chatSession: Log.lua:12-13 - global.chatTyped: History.lua:7 - global.whispers: LixardUI/Core/Whispers.lua:10 - profile.moduleBorders.chat: created on first use by Theme.lua:325-332 as {mode="default"}

Final page structure: sub-pages Window, Buttons, Typing, Messages, Whispers and history, Filters and alerts. Order comes from Options.lua:94-174.

9. Shared helper rows used by these modules

  • ns.Widgets.BorderSpecs(module) (LixardUI/Widgets/BorderSpecs.lua:22-58) gives 2 rows each: "Border color" (dropdown) and "Border color picker" (color). It is used by Chat, Friends, Vendor, Mail and Auction, so 10 rows in total. Every copy has the same label and desc. Data: profile.moduleBorders.. General has a global copy (General.lua:6, 247-248), and "Border color everywhere" resets them all (Theme.lua:371-384).
  • ns.Widgets.BarTextureSpec(store) (BarTexture.lua:79-97) gives 1 row each. It is used by Meters (meters.barTexture) and Tooltips (tooltips.barTexture, relabelled "Health bar texture"). The General copy is General.lua:120-133.
  • ns.TextStyles (TextStyle.lua) is not used by any of these 8 modules. Its GlobalSpecs (TextStyle.lua:226-243) puts 4 rows on General > Fonts, and the outline and shadow rows have no desc. Chat runs its own font and outline system instead (Chat.lua:81-86), with different outline values and labels.
  • Positions.lua and PlaceEditor.lua place text and elements inside unit and group frames. None of these 8 modules uses them, and they do not handle window position.

10. Problems found

10.1 Look settings repeated per module

  1. The border pair appears 5 times with the same label and desc (Chat Options.lua:4/235, Friends Options.lua:4/176, Vendor Options.lua:6/50, Mail Options.lua:6/77, Auction Options.lua:6/50). It also lands in a different sub-page each time (General.lua:270-293 HOMES): - Chat goes to Window. - Friends goes to Friends. - Vendor goes to View. - Mail and Auction go to General.
  2. Tooltips borders work differently. Tooltips has its own border key tooltips.border with options default/accent/quality/theme, and no color picker, class or none (Tooltips/Options.lua:92-104). The other modules use moduleBorders.* through BorderSpecs (default/theme/custom/class/none). So "Accent" here means what "Color" means elsewhere. Theme:SetBorderEverywhere has to special-case it (Theme.lua:376-377).
  3. Background opacity uses three different scales: - Chat: 0-1 with % format (Options.lua:176-184). - Tooltips: 40-100 with no % format (Tooltips/Options.lua:106-113). - Meters: only an on/off "Background" switch (Meters/Options.lua:192). - Friends, Vendor, Mail and Auction have no opacity setting at all.
  4. Fonts are split across three layers. - Chat has its own "Chat font" (lixardui/blizzard only) and "Text outline" (Options.lua:72-92, 214-231). The outline uses ""/OUTLINE/THICKOUTLINE with labels "Thin"/"Thick". TextStyles uses none/OUTLINE/THICKOUTLINE/MONOCHROME/MONOOUTLINE with labels "Thin outline"/"Thick outline" (TextStyle.lua:21-27). - The chat edit box ignores the outline (Chat.lua:84). - Chat font size comes only from Blizzard's tab menu (Options.lua:217 desc). - Meters "Text size" is an absolute 8-16 value (Meters/Options.lua:91), while General's Text size and Frame text size are percentages.
  5. Size and scale wording is inconsistent. - Tooltip "Scale" is a raw 0.6-1.6 factor (Tooltips/Options.lua:34-41). - Map waypoint "Size" is a percentage (Maps/Options.lua:80). - World map scale is done through QoL Ctrl-scroll (QoL/Move.lua:104-107). The maps.scale key is dead (see 10.5).
  6. Bar texture. The Meters texture row also controls interrupt-tracker bars (Kicks.lua:184), but it lives under Meters > Layout, not under Interrupts.

10.3 Settings that also exist in Blizzard windows or in-window menus

  • Chat (Blizzard):
  • Font size: tab right-click > Font Size (FCF_SetChatWindowFontSize, hooked at Chat.lua:558).
  • Background colour and opacity: tab menu > Background. This overlaps "Background opacity".
  • Lock and unlock: tab menu > Lock. It does not hide the grip; see 10.7.
  • Width, height and position: the Blizzard Edit Mode Chat Frame system.
  • Timestamps: Blizzard Options > Social > Chat Timestamps. The desc at OptionsMore.lua:158 admits it.
  • Sticky channels: Blizzard ChatTypeInfo sticky.
  • Fade: Blizzard's per-window FCF fading.
  • Chat style IM/Classic: Blizzard Social options. This overlaps "Typing box position".
  • The page description (Options.lua:97) tells the user to right-click a tab for Blizzard's own options.
  • Chat in-window: the Copy window has a "Plain text" switch (Copy.lua:130-131).
  • Friends window:
  • The header gear only opens Settings (Window.lua:688-691).
  • The filter menu (PageFriends.lua:468-526) holds 11 settings that are on no Settings page: showOffline, showApp, showGames, showOtherWoW, showBusy, maxOnly, hideAFK, sortBy, sortBy2, groupByCat, catFilter.
  • Who tab: whoNoGuild (Who.lua:129).
  • Guild tab: showOffline (Guild.lua:735).
  • Keys filter menu: show.* (Keys.lua:858).
  • QuickChat: qcMode (QuickChat.lua:210).
  • Messages footer: Save whispers (Messages.lua:321).
  • The alert-rule and category radios in the context menus (Menus.lua:14-128) edit watch and catMembers.
  • Meters: the gear menu on every window (Windows.lua:435-462).
  • Maps: the WQ tab filter menu (WorldQuestsUI.lua:299-350).
  • Vendor: the view menu and toggle (Window.lua:325-343, 390-398) and the filter menu (Filters.lua:211-223).
  • Auction: the Browse filter menu (Browse.lua:439).

10.4 Inconsistent widgets, wording and grouping (Chat first)

  1. Chat sub-pages are grouped wrong. - The "Typing" sub has "Clickable web links" in it (Options.lua:143-149). - The two typing-box rows sit in "Window" (Options.lua:110-124) instead of "Typing".
  2. Chat's "Whispers and history" sub holds only "Keep chat after reload". Its whisper half is moved to Friends by home="friends" (OptionsMore.lua:143, Options.lua:169-174).
  3. Chat's "Whispers" button depends on a setting on another page. Its desc says it "Only shows while Save whispers is on" (Options.lua:65), but that switch now lives on Friends > Whispers.
  4. The same label is used for two rows on one card. "Remove a word" appears for both blocked words and keywords (OptionsMore.lua:74, via WordSpecs at lines 133-138).
  5. The same label means different things on different pages. - "Alert sound" on Chat lets you pick the sound (OptionsMore.lua:248-269). - "Alert sound" on Friends uses a fixed toast (Friends/Options.lua:48-53, Alerts.lua:169). - "Keywords" names Chat's keyword alerts (input + pick + clear trio, OptionsMore.lua:136-138) and also Friends' invite keywords (one button with a prompt, Invites.lua:539-553). It is the same comma-list idea built with two different widget patterns.
  6. One list, two widget types. The Chat Buttons list mixes switches with auto/always/never dropdowns (Options.lua:52-68), and "Copy" uses a different key shape: copyButton instead of buttons.copy (Options.lua:55-64).
  7. disabledNote is used unevenly. - Present: Chat WhisperTab, Maps, Mail, Vendor. - Missing:
    • Chat: Fade after, Repeat window, Alert on my name, Alert sound, Alert sound to play (OptionsMore.lua:191, 225, 243, 254, 268).
    • Friends: "Only friends and guild" (Invites.lua:530).
    • Meters: NotOurs rows (Meters/Options.lua:53-79).
    • Kicks: icon rows (Kicks.lua:643-644).
    • Auction: Percent under (Auction/Options.lua:44).
  8. A dependent row that is never disabled. Maps "How see-through" stays enabled when fade is off (Maps/Options.lua:149).
  9. Vendor grid rows ignore list mode. Grid columns and Grid rows are not disabled while in List mode (Vendor/Options.lua:42-43).
  10. Section titles are prefixed on some pages only. "Vendor: repair", "Vendor: sell", "Mail: Open all" and "Mail: kinds to open" (Vendor/Options.lua:107-108, Mail/Options.lua:82-83) carry the module name; the other pages do not.
  11. Title Case in an in-window menu. "Show Housing Owned Count" (Vendor/Filters.lua:214) is Title Case, while the settings row reads "Show how many housing items you own" (Vendor/Options.lua:45).
  12. A destructive button without danger styling. "Clear the change log" has confirm but is not kind="danger" (GuildSocial.lua:185-193). Every other clear or delete button is danger-styled.
  13. Too many sub-pages for Tooltips. Tooltips ends up with 5 sidebar sub-pages, two of them with a single row (Backgrounds, Look). The page defines no sub, so Index (Options.lua:122-131) turns every look section into its own sub-page.
  14. An orphan "General" sub on Maps. The Maps "World quests" section has no sub (Maps/Options.lua:102-105), so it lands in a sub-page called "General" between Waypoint and Fade.
  15. A dead view entry. The QoL "Auctions and crafting" view (LixardUI_Options/Options.lua:74) is now empty: both of its sections moved to Auction (QoL/Options.lua:72, 373). BuildViews skips it silently (Options.lua:238-241).
  16. Mini panel keys are stored with keystones. They live in the global.friendsKeys defaults (Keys.lua:22-24), but the code is MiniPanel.lua.
  17. Friends filter keys are named backwards. maxOnly and hideAFK mean "count as busy" (PageFriends.lua:485-487, Data.lua:196-197), which is the opposite of what the names suggest.

10.5 Default keys never read, or used without a default

  • profile.maps.scale = 100 (Maps/Data.lua:10) is never read anywhere in the repo. grep "\.scale\b" LixardUI_Maps returns nothing. Map scale lives in profile.qol.scales.WorldMapFrame.
  • profile.meters.numbers and profile.meters.percent (Meters/Data.lua:29-30) are read only as legacy fallbacks (Windows.lua:34-38) and have no UI.
  • profile.chat.history = 1000 (Chat.lua:17) is used (Chat.lua:483-484) but has no UI.
  • These keys are used with no declared default: chat.width, chat.height, chat.copyPlain, friends.point, friendsKeys.miniPoint, friendsGuild.welcomeText, friendsGuild.congratsText, vendor.position, vendor.bagSellMerged, mail.pos, auction.pos, meters.barTexture, tooltips.barTexture.
  • Every other default key in all 8 modules was checked by grep and is read.

10.6 Rows with no desc

  • Meters Kicks: Width, Bar height, Spacing, Show kick icon, Icon size, Space after icon (Kicks.lua:639-644).
  • Vendor: "Show items as" (Options.lua:41), "Sell gray items" (Options.lua:101).
  • Mail: all 7 "kinds of mail" switches (Options.lua:46-52).
  • Auction: "Percent under" (Options.lua:38-45); QoL row "Prices from", shown on Auction (QoL/Options.lua:446-456).
  • Shared: TextStyle GlobalSpecs "Frame text outline" and "Frame text shadow" (TextStyle.lua:237-238). These are on General, not on these pages.

10.7 Bugs noticed while tracing get/set

  • The Chat resize grip ignores the lock. Its desc says it is "Hidden while the window is locked" (Options.lua:128). But UpdateGrip checks only resizeGrip (Mover.lua:91-94), even though it is hooked to FCF_SetLocked (Mover.lua:170-172).
  • Some Mail setters skip the change hooks. "Keep bag slots free" and the category switches do not call OpenAllChanged or LIXARDUI_SETTINGS_CHANGED (Mail/Options.lua:36, 49).
  • The tooltip mover popup can miss a row (likely). The popup builds its rows before it loads Options (Dialog.lua:105-106). "Background opacity" is only moved into T.Page when General.lua runs. So the first popup opened before the settings window can lack that row, and the panel is then cached (Dialog.lua:116).

11. Window position and size: where stored, who writes, how it relates to the mover

Window Position key Size key Written by In /lui move (Layout)?
Chat (ChatFrame1) profile.layouts.Default.chat {point,x,y} or an anchor link (Layout.lua:297-321) profile.chat.width / height Mover drag; resize grip (Mover.lua:97-109, saves size and calls Layout:Move); mover popup sliders (Mover.lua:29-44). No row on the Settings page Yes, key "chat" (Mover.lua:147-158)
Friends window global.friends.point {point,rel,x,y} (Window.lua:650-657, 678-682) global.friends.width / height (default 380x560, Data.lua:48-49) Its own drag and resize only No. It is also the only window stored in global scope instead of profile
Friends mini panel global.friendsKeys.miniPoint (MiniPanel.lua:33-37, 98-102) fixed Own drag; "Lock its position" row (MiniPanel.lua:219-225) No
Meter windows profile.layouts.Default.meterN profile.meters.windows[i].width/rows (grip, Windows.lua:608-639); global meters.width/rows Drag and grip call Layout:Move (Windows.lua:600-606, 633-639). Options rows "Width" and "Bars" also write size and wipe per-window sizes (Options.lua:31-34, 88-89). "Stack the windows" writes positions (Options.lua:72-80) Yes, "meter1".."meterN" (Windows.lua:790-794)
Interrupt tracker profile.layouts.Default.kicks profile.kicks.width / barHeight Options rows and popup (same specs) Yes, "kicks" (Kicks.lua:652-655)
Tooltip anchor profile.layouts.Default.tooltip - (scale = tooltips.scale) Mover only; used only when tooltips.anchor = "mover" Yes, "tooltip" (Unit.lua:290-293)
World map profile.qol.positions.WorldMapFrame (QoL/Move.lua:35-42) profile.qol.scales.WorldMapFrame (Ctrl-scroll, Move.lua:104-107) QoL only; nothing on the Maps page (maps.scale is dead) No
Vendor window profile.vendor.position {point,relativePoint,x,y} (Window.lua:253-266) profile.vendor.sizes[vendorKey] {w,h} (Window.lua:489-497); computed from gridColumns, gridRows and autoSize (Window.lua:82-103) Own drag and grip. Options rows Grid columns, Grid rows and Fit-to-items change the size No
Mailbox profile.mail.pos {x,y} (Mail/Window.lua:19-38), applied to Blizzard's MailFrame fixed Own drag. QoL Move is skipped while enabled (Mail/Window.lua:173-174) No
Auction house profile.auction.pos {x,y} (Auction/Window.lua:22-38) fixed Own drag. QoL skip at Auction/Window.lua:267-268 No

So these 8 modules use four separate position systems: Layout profile.layouts, ad-hoc per-module point/pos/position keys in three different shapes, QoL positions and scales, and global-scope Friends.

Chat specifically: Edit Mode versus /lui move

  • Mover setup. The mover is registered with keepDefault = true (Mover.lua:148).
  • While there is no saved spot, Position returns the frame's current offset (Layout.lua:166-168), and Apply only shows coordinates without moving the frame (Layout.lua:231-240).
  • So until the first LixardUI save, Blizzard's own chat position is authoritative. That means Blizzard Edit Mode's Chat Frame system, or FCF dragging of an unlocked chat.
  • Two systems in Edit Mode. Layout turns its movers on when Blizzard Edit Mode opens (EventRegistry "EditMode.Enter", Layout.lua:1158-1161, 1201-1202). So in Edit Mode the user sees both Blizzard's Chat Frame selection and the LixardUI "Chat" mover. The page text, "Move chat in Edit Mode or with /lui move; click it there for width and height" (Chat/Options.lua:97), does not say which one.
  • After the first LixardUI save, LixardUI wins.
  • A SetPoint hook (Mover.lua:162-169) re-applies the saved spot one frame after any Blizzard reposition. A Blizzard Edit Mode or FCF move then snaps back silently.
  • Layout:Register hooks OnSizeChanged (Layout.lua:987-993). That calls Apply, then onApply = ApplySize (Mover.lua:13-18, 155), which re-sets chat.width/height whenever they are saved.
  • So once the grip or the mover sliders have been used, Blizzard's Edit Mode width and height sliders and Blizzard's resize no longer stick.
  • Reset is incomplete. Layout:Reset (Layout.lua:381-391) clears only the position spot, not chat.width/height. Because of keepDefault, Apply after a reset does not move the frame either.
  • Width and height are not on the Settings page. They exist only in the mover popup (Mover.lua:27-44, settings = SizeSpecs). The Chat page has no size rows, but it does duplicate three of the popup's other rows: typing box, background opacity and tab opacity.

Part 2

Settings audit: LixardUI_UnitFrames + LixardUI_GroupFrames

Read-only audit, version 0.0.854. Nothing in the repo was changed.

Files read: LixardUI_UnitFrames/*.toc, Data.lua, Options.lua, Frames.lua, CastBars.lua, Extras.lua, Swing.lua, Auras.lua, parts of Resources.lua. LixardUI_GroupFrames/*.toc, Data.lua, Options.lua, Buttons.lua (1-490, 830-850), Headers.lua (300-594), Tanks.lua. Core: Core/Entry.lua, Core/Modules.lua, Core/Database.lua, Theme/Theme.lua (315-380), Widgets/BorderSpecs.lua, BarTexture.lua, TextStyle.lua, AuraHide.lua, RoleIcon.lua, HealthExtras.lua (770-1046), Positions.lua, PlaceEditor.lua, Layout/Layout.lua, Layout/Dialog.lua, LixardUI_Options/Options.lua (Index/BuildPage), Pages/Extra.lua, Pages/General.lua (look-spec routing).

File order: UF = Data, Frames, Auras, Resources, Swing, EbonProbe, CastBars, Extras, Preview, Options, Init. GF = Data, Buttons, Headers, Tanks, Preview, Options, Init. Both are LoadOnDemand and depend on LixardUI. Core/Modules.lua:8-28 loads UnitFrames before GroupFrames.

How the page gets put together (needed to read the tables)

  • UF calls ns:AddOptionsPage(page) with key unitframes (UF/Options.lua:771). AddOptionsPage runs RetailOnly, which copies every section.specs into a new table and drops foreverOnly/retailOnly items (Core/Entry.lua:15-32).
  • After building the page, UF moves every section whose tab == "Auras" into a sub-page called Auras, with the unit name as the tab (UF/Options.lua:724).
  • GF registers page groupframes with into = "unitframes" (GF/Options.lua:637). Pages/Extra.lua:8-17 adds its sections to the end of the unitframes page, so in the UI everything is one page.
  • ns:AddLookSpecs(title, specs, sub) rows are placed by Pages/General.lua:271-324. The HOMES table sends "Class resource", "Encounter bar", "Unit, party and raid frames", "Health", "Reaction and power" and "Cast bars" back into the unitframes page. Each lands as a section with no tab. Its title is the kind ("Bars", "Backgrounds", "Borders and corners", "Colors"), or the entry title when a section with that kind title already exists.
  • In a sub-page that has tabs, a section without a tab goes into a tab called General (LixardUI_Options/Options.lua:146).
  • A spec with advanced = true is moved into one "Advanced" card per sub-page (LixardUI_Options/Options.lua:595-637).
  • Shared row generators:
  • TS:Specs(id,label) makes 8 rows (Widgets/TextStyle.lua:143-224): <label>: Font, : Size, : Outline, : Shadow, : Color, : Custom color, : Show, : Reset this text. They are stored at profile.textStyles[id].{font,size,outline,shadow,colorMode,color,shown}. None of them has a desc.
  • W.RoleSpecs makes 12 rows (Widgets/RoleIcon.lua:134-159).
  • W.SharedHealthSpecs makes 21 rows (Widgets/HealthExtras.lua:843-930 plus 798-819).
  • W.AuraHideSpecs makes 4 rows. W.AuraHideScopeSpecs makes 2 (Widgets/AuraHide.lua:126-180).
  • W.BorderSpecs(module) makes 2 rows (Widgets/BorderSpecs.lua:22-57). They are stored at profile.moduleBorders[module].{mode,color}.
  • W.BarTextureSpec makes 1 row (Widgets/BarTexture.lua:153-171).

Abbreviations: uf = profile.unitframes, gf = profile.groupframes, P = profile. <u> = the UF unit key (player, target, targettarget, focus, pet, boss, arena). <k> = the GF kind (party, raid, raid30, raid40, tanks, assists, raidpets). "n/d" means the row has no desc. "adv" means advanced = true.

Extra rows when UnitFrames is NOT loaded

3. Problems found

3a. The same SV key, or the same visible effect, controlled from two places

  1. GF role-icon toggles are lost when UF is loaded. GF/Options.lua:627-629 appends the six "On party frames…" switches to the cached shared table returned by W.SharedRoleSpecs() (Widgets/RoleIcon.lua:169-176). By that time UF has already registered its page, and ns:AddOptionsPage replaced section.specs with a copy (Core/Entry.lua:15-29). UF loads first (Core/Modules.lua:10-11), so in the normal setup gf.<k>.roleIcon cannot be reached from any page. Its reader is Buttons.lua:222.
  2. Two ways to hide each text. Each pair hides the same text through two different keys: - uf.<u>.showName ("Show name", UF/Options.lua:279, and again in the mover through FrameSpecs :186-191) vs textStyles["uf.<u>.name"].shown ("Name: Show", TextStyle.lua:215-221). Both sit on the same Texts tab. - uf.<u>.showLevel (Frame tab, :146-151) vs "Level: Show" (Texts tab). - uf.<u>.healthText = "none" vs "Health: Show". - uf.<u>.powerText = "none" vs "Power: Show". - GF: gf.<k>.healthText = "none" (Frame tab :100) vs "Health text: Show" (Texts tab).

So 4 pairs x 7 UF units and 1 pair x 6 GF kinds. 3. Three or four sizes for one text. - UF: uf.textSize (General > Text, :637-643), textStyles["uf.<u>.<part>"].size (x28 sliders), P.frameText.scale and P.fonts.scale (General page, Pages/General.lua:104-116). - GF: gf.<k>.textSize (Frame tab GF/Options.lua:99) and "Name: Size" (Texts tab, same sub-page). Once the user moves "Name: Size", the Frame-tab slider stops affecting the name. - Class resource has a third size of its own, centerResources.textSize (UF/Options.lua:475). Its font is hard-coded at Resources.lua:516 (Theme:Font("normal"), "OUTLINE"), so it ignores frameText.font/outline and TextStyles. 4. The UF per-text Size slider shows the wrong value. - Frames.lua:629 attaches the styles without a def, so TS:Specs DefaultSize returns 12 (TextStyle.lua:158-162). - The real size comes from uf.textSize, and power uses textSize-2 (Frames.lua:594-598). - With the defaults, every "Power: Size" slider says 12 while the text renders at 10. - The "Text size" desc says "name, level and health" and does not mention that power is 2 smaller (:639). 5. Corners. uf.corners is written by UF General > Borders and corners (UF/Options.lua:727-737) and by General > "Corners" everywhere (Pages/General.lua:225-238, through Theme.lua:339-361). That function also writes gf.corners. When UF is absent, the GF per-kind Look rows write gf.<k>.corners (GF/Options.lua:521), and "everywhere" does not clear those, so they win. 6. Border. P.moduleBorders.unitframes is written by: - UF General (UF/Options.lua:9, 759). - All six GF per-kind Look tabs when UF is absent (GF/Options.lua:519): it looks per-kind but is one key. - General "Apply to all" (Pages/General.lua:241-251, Theme.lua:370-375).

The same border is drawn on GF buttons, both modules' cast bars and UF aura icons (Buttons.lua:465, 955, 1177, CastBars.lua:48, Auras.lua:65). 7. Look keys GF never reads while UF is loaded. - UnitLook (Buttons.lua:135-149) makes corners, healthColor, customHealth, brightness and barTexture come from uf whenever ns.UnitFrames exists. BarSource takes texture and brightness from uf for every field. - Any gf.<k>.* look values saved while UF was off are silently ignored once UF is turned back on. - gf.matchUnitBars (Data.lua:13) does not turn this sharing off. It only gates a relayout (UF/Frames.lua:744), and no row exposes it. 8. Power bar. gf.<k>.power and gf.<k>.powerHeight have one row on the "Bars and range" tab (GF/Options.lua:558-559). The same rows are made again by FrameSpecs(kind) (:103-104) and shown in the party mover (:338) and on raidpets. That is the same key from two generators, through two different local functions both named KindSpec (:83 and :496, the second shadows the first). 9. Unit width/height/text rows also appear in the mover dialog. - U.MoverSpecs (UF/Options.lua:549-552) picks FrameSpecs rows by index 2-9. - U.ResourceMoverSpecs (:437-440) also picks by index. - G.MoverSpecs (GF/Options.lua:311-347) picks by label text.

This works, but it breaks as soon as a row is added or reordered. 10. Health color. uf.healthColor is set by its dropdown (:554-561) and also forced to "custom" by the color picker (:573). The GF copy does the same (GF/Options.lua:540). The text-color picker in TS works the same way (TextStyle.lua:212). 11. Bar texture has five different keys, and the rows are scattered: - P.barTexture on General > Bars. - uf.barTexture on UF General > Bars. - P.castBarTexture on UF General > "Cast bars". It is a profile-level key that also drives GF cast bars. - uf.centerResources.texture on the Class resource > General tab. - uf.altPower.barTexture on Extras > General tab. The same spec also appears in the encounter-bar mover (UF/Options.lua:520).

The encounter bar texture is on a different tab from the encounter bar's other settings.

3b. Look settings repeated per unit or module

  • Font, size, outline, shadow, color, custom color, show and reset per text.
  • UF: 4 texts x 7 units = 28 blocks of 8 = 224 rows.
  • GF: 2 x 6 kinds + party target = 13 blocks = 104 rows.
  • That is 328 rows that mostly repeat P.frameText (Pages/General.lua:116).
  • There is no "copy to all units" option and no shared per-module default between global and per-text.
  • Text size. uf.textSize (1), gf.<k>.textSize (7 including raidpets), centerResources.textSize, plus each TS Size.
  • Bar texture. UF has its own local copy of the spec (UF/Options.lua:618-625) and GF has another (GF/Options.lua:529). Both duplicate W.BarTextureSpec (BarTexture.lua:153) with different desc text and options. The shared helper is already used at :450, :520, :524 and :769.
  • Brightness. UF/Options.lua:607-616 is advanced = true. The GF copy at GF/Options.lua:528 is not advanced.
  • Colors. Health color lists differ:
  • U.HEALTH_COLORS ("Class or reaction", "Dark (class color text)", Frames.lua:51-55).
  • G.HEALTH_COLORS ("Class color", "Dark (class color names)", Buttons.lua:104-108).
  • The descs also differ (UF/Options.lua:557 vs GF/Options.lua:527).
  • Health text options differ. UF has 7 choices, GF has 5, and "value"/"percent" are in a different order (Frames.lua:35-43 vs Buttons.lua:90-96).
  • Corners and border. Copied per GF kind (x6) when UF is absent.
  • Scale. Only the class resource has a Scale slider (UF/Options.lua:432). No unit or group frame has one, which is inconsistent the other way.
  • Raid-marker size. Only on target sub-frames (GF/Options.lua:293, 443). UF hard-codes 18 (Frames.lua:634).

3c. Inconsistent widget choices

  • No type = "check" anywhere in these modules or their helpers. All toggles are switches, so that is consistent.
  • On/off for aura rows:
  • UF uses the position dropdown with an "Off" value (Auras.lua:15-21, UF/Options.lua:209-216).
  • GF uses a "Show …" switch plus a separate "spot" dropdown (GF/Options.lua:179-197).
  • Shadow.
  • Per-text Shadow is a 3-state dropdown (TextStyle.lua:183-195).
  • Global "Frame text shadow" is a switch (TextStyle.lua:238).
  • Per-text "Show" is a switch.
  • Text position:
  • Class resource uses its own Left/Center/Right list TEXT_SPOTS (UF/Options.lua:402-406).
  • Unit and group frames use W.PlaceOptions (23 or 9 positions).
  • GF cast bar and aura spots use subsets (Buttons.lua:396-405, 444-451).
  • Color dropdown plus picker. Each pairing exists in at least two shapes:
  • Health color + Health bar color.
  • Class resource Color + Bar color.
  • Main power color + Main power bar color.
  • TS Color + Custom color.
  • Border color + Border color picker.
  • Role icon color + 3 swatches.

Some pickers force the mode to custom when you pick a color (UF/Options.lua:573, GF/Options.lua:540, TextStyle.lua:212, BorderSpecs.lua:54). Others do not (ResourceColor, UF/Options.lua:373-388; role swatches; extraColors). - Advanced is barely used. 1 of about 1084 rows. Good candidates for advanced: every TS block, Name width, the Layout tabs, and all 21 Health bar pattern rows.

3d. Inconsistent naming and wording

  • The "use" switch:
  • "Use the LixardUI %s" (UF/Options.lua:104).
  • "Use the LixardUI cast bar" (:315).
  • "Use LixardUI party frames" without "the" (GF/Options.lua:389, 464).
  • "Use tank frames" without "LixardUI" (:353-355).
  • "Class resource bar", "Encounter bar", "Totem and summon timers" and "Swing timer" all act as "use" switches without saying so.
  • Cast bar:
  • UF: "Use the LixardUI cast bar", "Height", "Spell icon", "Time left" (UF/Options.lua:315-348).
  • GF: "Show cast bars", "Cast bar height", "Spell icon", "Time left" (GF/Options.lua:221-225).
  • "Time left": UF auras say "Time left" (:250), the swing timer says "Show time left" (Swing.lua:328).
  • Aura rows:
  • UF: "Icon size", "Icons per row", "Rows", "Only mine".
  • GF: "Debuff icon size", "Most debuffs", "Which debuffs". GF has no per-row or row-count control, and UF has no "max".
  • Spacing:
  • "Space between frames" (GF).
  • "Gap between points" (resource, UF/Options.lua:431).
  • "Spacing" (totems, :513).
  • "Space above main power" (:484).
  • "Distance from the frame" / "Distance from party frames" (GF/Options.lua:269, 421).
  • Raid marker: "Raid marker" (switch, UF Icons / GF Icons, and also a Layout dropdown with the same label) vs "Show raid marker" (GF/Options.lua:292, 442). The same label is used for both an on/off switch and a position dropdown.
  • Role icon per frame: the UF key is showRole, label "Show role icon" (UF/Options.lua:153-157). The GF key is roleIcon, label "On party frames" (GF/Options.lua:598-619). Same concept, different key and wording.
  • Opacity: "Opacity" 10-100 (UF/Options.lua:433), "Faded opacity" 0-100 (:421), "Background opacity" (:456), "Out of range" (GF/Options.lua:560, which is also an opacity), "Ring opacity" 20-100.
  • Section titles:
  • "Raid 20 frame" / "Raid up to 20 size" / "Raid up to 20 texts" / "Raid 20 layout" / "Raid up to 20" (GF/Options.lua:51-69, 651).
  • "Tank frame" vs "Tanks" vs "Tank".
  • "Main assist" vs "Assists".
  • Section titles reused for different content: "Party targets" (Size title, :63) vs "Party target layout" vs "Party target text".
  • Slider ranges for the same thing differ:
  • Width: UF 120-400 vs GF 40-300.
  • Height: UF 20-100 vs GF 16-100.
  • Cast height: UF 10-40 vs GF 4-20.
  • Text size: 8-18 vs 6-24 vs 6-32.
  • Desc wording for the same row:
  • Health text: "What the number on the health bar shows…" (UF/Options.lua:165) vs "Number on the frame…" (GF/Options.lua:100).
  • Corners: "Clipped or square corners on unit, party and raid frames." vs "Clipped or square frames." (:730 vs GF:521).

3e. Default keys in Data.lua that nothing reads

Grep commands (run from the repo root):

  • grep -rnE "[.:]KEY\b|\[\"KEY\"\]|\"KEY\"" LixardUI_UnitFrames --include=*.lua | grep -v Data.lua (and the same for LixardUI_GroupFrames), looped over every key in both Data.lua files.
  • Follow-ups:
  • grep -rnwE "heals" --include=*.lua .
  • grep -rnE "\bresources\b" LixardUI_UnitFrames
  • grep -rnE "G:DB\(\)\.(shield|tint|tintStyle|tintStrength|shieldFill|overshield|healAbsorb|absorbs|heals)" .
  • grep -rn "raid40Ready\|raid30Ready\|matchUnitBars\|roleIcon\b" .
  • grep -rnE "profile\.unitframes|profile\.groupframes|castBarTexture" . (outside the two modules).
Default File:line Status
uf.resources (whole table: enabled, position, height, gap, extraMana, mainPower) UF/Data.lua:63 Dead. Only mentioned as a key name in LIVE_SWITCHES (Frames.lua:799), which exists only to skip it.
uf.shield, overshield, healAbsorb, tint, tintStyle, shieldFill, tintStrength UF/Data.lua:56-62 Dead defaults. Only read by the one-time migration through rawget (HealthExtras.lua:1023-1026), which never sees defaults. The live values are in P.healthExtras.
gf.shield, overshield, healAbsorb, tint, tintStyle, shieldFill, tintStrength GF/Data.lua:21-27 Dead. Never read. HealthBarSettings only migrates from unitframes. Headers.lua:561 still writes gf.shield = "off", which is a dead write.
gf.heals GF/Data.lua:19 Dead (0 hits outside Data.lua).
gf.absorbs GF/Data.lua:20 Only read with rawget in a migration (Headers.lua:561). The default is dead.
gf.matchUnitBars GF/Data.lua:13 Read once (UF/Frames.lua:744), no row, always true. A hidden switch that does less than its name says (see 3a.7).
gf.corners, healthColor, customHealth, brightness, barTexture GF/Data.lua:8-12 Only used when UF is not loaded (Buttons.lua:135-149).
gf.icons.* role keys (written at Headers.lua:562-563) db.icons.roleDamager = true writes into gf.icons, which no longer drives role icons (moved to P.roleIcons, RoleIcon.lua:15-30). Dead write.
uf..offsets UF/Data.lua:26 Legacy, still read in U.PlaceFor (Frames.lua:86). OK.
uf..debuffs/buffs.spacing, uf.swing.gap UF/Data.lua:8, 69 Read (CastBars.lua:23, Swing.lua:102) but have no row. Not dead.
gf.raid40Ready, raid30Ready GF/Data.lua:38-39 Migration flags. Read.

3f. Option rows with no desc

Template counts. Multiply by the repeats.

  • UF:
  • Every TS:Specs row: 8 per block x 28 = 224.
  • Show name (:279).
  • Power text (:293).
  • Resource rows: Show (:416), Main power when there's no class resource (:423), Width/Height/Gap/Scale/Opacity (:429-433), Color and Bar color (:444-445), Background opacity (:456), the 4 text dropdowns and Text size (:464-475), Main power height/space/color/bar color (:483-486).
  • Swing: Show it as, Bar height, Ring size, Ring opacity (Swing.lua:324-330).
  • Bar colors reset (:594).
  • RoleSpecs: Tanks, Healers, Damage, style, fill, Size on party and raid, Role icon color, 3 swatches (RoleIcon.lua:136-158).
  • Incoming heal look (HealthExtras.lua:908).
  • The 10 extra color swatches (HealthExtras.lua:799-808).
  • Longer than (minutes) (AuraHide.lua:157).
  • GF:
  • Every TS row: 8 x 13 = 104.
  • Which debuffs, all icon-size and Most rows, Glow for (GF/Options.lua:180-199, 10 per kind).
  • Cast bar height, Spell icon, Time left (:222-225).
  • ExtraSizeSpecs, 4 rows (:246-251).
  • ExtraTargetSpecs: target's target, Width, Height, Distance (:266-269).
  • Raid marker size (:293, :443).
  • Tags (:366).
  • Party target Width/Height/Distance (:419-421).
  • RoleKindSpecs (:611).
  • In total, roughly 550 of the ~1084 rows have no desc, most of them from TS:Specs.

3g. Structure problems

  • Auras are in a different place in each module. UF auras are under their own Auras sub-page (UF/Options.lua:724). GF auras stay on each kind's "Auras" tab (GF/Options.lua:207-209). GF/Options.lua:708 even checks for sub == "Auras", which GF never produces.
  • The "Pet" sub-page holds two different things. UF's player-pet frame (UF/Options.lua:675-680) and GF's raid pets (GF/Options.lua:690-693, tab "Raid pets") share the "Pet" sub-page.
  • Look sections end up on a "General" tab. The look specs routed into Class resource and Extras have no tab, so they get a "General" tab (LixardUI_Options/Options.lua:146). That gives "Class resource > General" (texture, background, border) and "Extras > General" (the encounter bar texture), next to the content tabs (Pages/General.lua:286-287, 309).
  • Raid pets have no Texts tab. Raidpets get Frame and Layout but no Texts, Bars, Icons or Auras tabs (KIND_TABS :583-590 leaves raidpets out).

4. Position and size: where they are stored, and how the mover fits in

Position is stored only by the mover system: profile.layouts[<layoutName>][<moverKey>].

  • <layoutName> is "Default", or the largest saved layout (Layout.lua:24-38).
  • The value is { point, x, y }, or for an attached frame { anchor, side, x, y, fallback } (Layout.lua:296-316).
  • The mover dialog's "Left / right" and "Up / down" boxes and "Reset position" write the same table (Layout/Dialog.lua:76-86).
  • Defaults come from the defaultX/defaultY arguments to Layout:Register, not from Data.lua.

Mover keys:

  • UF:
  • player, target, targettarget, focus, pet (Frames.lua:763-769, 826).
  • boss, arena: one mover per stack of 5 (:772-775, 788).
  • playerCast, targetCast, targettargetCast, focusCast (CastBars.lua:56). These default to pinned under the frame or resource bar (:87-102). Boss and arena cast bars are parented to their frames and have no mover.
  • centerResources (Resources.lua:779), totems (Extras.lua:92), encounterBar (Extras.lua:167).
  • GF:
  • party.
  • raid: one mover for all three raid brackets (Headers.lua:418-421, 489). Raid 20, 30 and 40 have separate sizes but share one position. G.MoverPage/G.MoverSpecs send the dialog to the current bracket (GF/Options.lua:324, 329-333).
  • tankFrames, tankFrames2..4, assistFrames, assistFrames2..4, raidPetFrames (Tanks.lua:9-21, 147, 173).

Size is stored in the module settings, not in layouts: uf.<u>.width/height, uf.<u>Cast.width/height, uf.centerResources.width/height/scale, uf.totems.size, uf.altPower.width/height, gf.<k>.width/height/gap/groupGap/perRow, gf.<k>.targetWidth/targetHeight.

  • No options row writes x/y. Position is mover-only.
  • Options rows do write width and height, and the same rows are reused in the mover's settings panel through settings = function() return …MoverSpecs end:
  • UF frames: U.MoverSpecs = Width, Height, Power bar height, Health text, One decimal, Power text, Show name, Portrait (UF/Options.lua:549-552).
  • Cast bars: U.CastSpecs (CastBars.lua:57).
  • Resource: enabled, Show, Faded opacity, W, H, Gap, Scale, Opacity (:437-440).
  • Totems/encounter bar: U.ExtraSpecs (Extras.lua:93, 168). The encounter-bar mover also shows Bar texture.
  • GF: SizeSpecs plus picked rows (GF/Options.lua:328-347).

So each size slider exists twice (page and mover dialog) with one key. That is consistent, but it relies on index/label picking (3a.9). - Size changes can move a frame. Layout:Register hooks OnSizeChanged and re-applies the saved position (Layout.lua:987-993), so resizing keeps the saved anchor point. For GF party and raid, Headers.lua:317-320 goes further: when width or height changes, it calls ns.Layout:Move, which writes a new saved position (Layout.lua:377-379 → Store) to keep the top-left corner in place. A size slider on the options page can therefore silently create or overwrite profile.layouts[..].party/raid, and with it the shared raid position for all three brackets. - Positions inside a frame (texts and icons) are a separate system: - Keys: uf.<u>.places[element] = { position, x, y } (Frames.lua:93-104), the legacy offsets, gf.<k>.places[...] (Buttons.lua:337-358), and the GF aura and cast spots gf.<k>.<aura>.place. - Written by both the Layout-tab dropdowns (which reset x/y to 0, UF/Options.lua:71, GF/Options.lua:118) and preview dragging through W.PlaceEditor (Widgets/PlaceEditor.lua:54-252, which snaps to the nearest spot using the W.POSITIONS table in Positions.lua). - These have nothing to do with the screen mover. Picking a spot from the dropdown throws away any drag offset.

Part 3

Settings audit: ActionBars, Cooldowns, DataBars, Auras

Read-only audit of LixardUI. Paths are relative to the repo root.

How the rows are placed (this decides what counts as a sub-page): - LixardUI_Options/Options.lua:122-157 Index(): if no section has sub and the page has 2 or more sections, each section becomes its own sub-page, named after the section title. That applies to Action Bars. A section with no sub on a page that has subs goes to the "General" sub-page. A section with no tab in a sub that has tabs goes to the "General" tab. - ns:AddLookSpecs (LixardUI/Core/Entry.lua:11-13) rows are moved onto the module page by LixardUI_Options/Pages/General.lua:271-324 (HOMES + MoveHome). The section is titled after the kind ("Borders and corners" / "Bars"), or after the entry title if a section with the kind's name already exists in that sub. This runs before Pages/Extra.lua indexes the module pages, so the moved rows do get sub-pages. - advanced = true: Options.lua:595-640. Rows are split into an "Advanced" card per sub-page, except on a page with no sub where 2 or more sections have advanced rows (Action Bars), where they stay in place (keepAdvanced). The mover dialog (LixardUI/Layout/Dialog.lua:102-120) builds every spec and ignores advanced. - Mover dialogs reuse the same spec tables through options.settings (Dialog.lua:105), so every "mover" row below is a second UI for the same key.

SV root abbreviations: P. = ns.DB.profile., G. = ns.DB.global.

3. Problems found

3.1 Look settings every module repeats

  • Corners, four copies plus a global one. actionbars.corners (ActionBars/Options.lua:21-28), cooldowns.corners (Cooldowns/Options.lua:214-220), databars.corners (DataBars/Options.lua:289-303), auras.corners (Auras/Options.lua:91-101). General "Corners" (General.lua:225-237) overwrites all of them through Theme:SetCornersEverywhere (Theme.lua:338-357). Only Action Bars also has a radius (radius, ActionBars/Options.lua:42-51). The other three hard-code a radius: Cooldowns 3 (Manager.lua:44, Cooldowns.lua:46), Auras size/6 capped at 5 (Auras.lua:78), DataBars Theme:RoundRadius("small") (Bars.lua:355).
  • Border mode + color, four copies. ns.Widgets.BorderSpecs is used in ActionBars/Options.lua:14, Cooldowns/Options.lua:5, DataBars/Options.lua:6 and Auras/Options.lua:5. That gives 8 rows writing P.moduleBorders.<module>, on top of the General BorderSpec and its "Apply to all" button (General.lua:240-251, Theme.lua:371-376).
  • Border thickness exists only on Action Bars (borderSize, ActionBars/Options.lua:53-60). The others pass a fixed 1 to W.Panel (DataBars Bars.lua:372, Panels.lua:626).
  • Bar texture, three places. General P.barTexture (General.lua:121-133), P.databars.barTexture (DataBars/Options.lua:306, through W.BarTextureSpec), and P.cooldowns.manager.bars.barTexture (Cooldowns/Options.lua:223). Cooldowns rebuilds the spec by hand and copies BarTextureSpec's desc word for word (BarTexture.lua:84) instead of calling W.BarTextureSpec. In skin-only mode (manager off), flatBars forces a hard-coded BAR texture and ignores both settings (Cooldowns.lua:94-95).
  • Fonts are hard-coded everywhere. No module uses ns.TextStyles. ActionBars hotkey/count/macro use fixed sizes 11/13/10 with "OUTLINE" (Style.lua:33-43). Cooldowns uses Theme:Font("normal") + "OUTLINE" (Manager.lua:95-97, Preview.lua:47,53,74,78). Auras derives size from icon size with "OUTLINE" (Auras.lua:87-88). DataBars uses W.Text(..., 12) with no style id (Panels.lua:526, Bars.lua:443). So General > "Frame text font/outline/shadow/size", whose desc says it covers "text on unit frames, bars and other on-screen frames" (TextStyle.lua:235-241, shown at General.lua:116), does nothing for these four modules. Only Theme:FontScale reaches them.
  • Text size, four schemes. ActionBars has none. Cooldowns has per-viewer px numberSize/countSize/fontSize plus a global % numberScale (Options.lua:116-121,191). DataBars has px stored as font (Options.lua:93), and xp/rep/honor text size follows bar height (Bars.lua:360). Auras has none (derived).
  • Size and scale, mixed units. Pixel sliders: AB "Button size" (Options.lua:179-186), Cooldowns "Icon size" (Options.lua:56-68), Auras "Icon size" (Auras/Options.lua:37), vehicle "Button size" (Extra.lua:153-163). Percent scale sliders labelled just "Size": micro/bags (Micro.lua:289-301), queue eye (Eye.lua:32-44).

3.2 Same SV key controlled from two or more places

  1. actionbars.bars.barN.showGrid: "Show empty buttons on every bar" (ActionBars/Options.lua:101-116) and each bar's "Always show empty buttons" (Options.lua:233-239).
  2. <module>.corners for all four modules: the module row and General "Corners" (General.lua:225-237 -> Theme.lua:338-357).
  3. P.moduleBorders.<module>: the module BorderSpecs and General "Border color everywhere" (General.lua:240-251 -> Theme.lua:371-376, resets all to "default").
  4. cooldowns.manager.<essential|utility|buffs>.width: "Icon size" writes width and height (Cooldowns/Options.lua:61-66), and "Width" writes width (Options.lua:70). "Icon size" only reads height (Options.lua:60), so after moving Width the two rows disagree.
  5. AB layout keys buttons, perRow, growX, growY, size, spacing: per-bar sliders and the Match / Undo Match / Default bar layout buttons (Bars.lua:240-321).
  6. actionbars.microScale/bagsBarScale + P.layouts.*.microMenu/bagsBar: mover dialog (Micro.lua:286-304) and /lui micro reset (Commands.lua:381-386 -> Micro.lua:499-523), which also resets micro/bagsBar to "style".
  7. P.auraHide.*: Auras page "Hidden" (Auras/Options.lua:87-89) and UnitFrames (UnitFrames/Options.lua:648). They are mutually exclusive by ns.UnitFrames, so the Auras page loses the section without explanation when UnitFrames is on.
  8. Every mover dialog is a second UI for the page rows (Bars.lua:332, Special.lua:198/203, Manager.lua:398, DataBars Bars.lua:452 + Panels.lua:640, Auras.lua:291). It ignores advanced (Dialog.lua:115-117), so the advanced "Buttons per row" shows in the bar mover but is hidden on the page.
  9. cooldowns.numbers / numberScale change countdown numbers on action buttons and items (desc at Cooldowns/Options.lua:190; code Cooldowns.lua:16-22), but there is no link from the Action Bars page.

3.3 Inconsistent widgets and wording

  • Corner label: "Corner style" (ActionBars/Options.lua:23) vs "Corners" (Cooldowns:214, DataBars:291, Auras:93, General:225).
  • Border label: AB "Icon border" (Options.lua:14), everyone else the default "Border color" (BorderSpecs.lua:26). The picker is "Border color picker" in all four, so AB pairs "Icon border" with "Border color picker".
  • Growth: AB "Buttons go" Right/Left (Options.lua:158-168), Cooldowns "Grow" Right/Left (Cooldowns:75-78), Auras "Grow" "To the left"/"To the right", left first (Auras.lua:35-38).
  • Row direction: AB "New rows go" lists Up, Down (Options.lua:172-175). Cooldowns lists Down, Up (Cooldowns:80-83). Auras has no row direction.
  • Per row: AB "Buttons per row" is advanced (Options.lua:151). Cooldowns/Auras "Icons per row" is not (Cooldowns:74, Auras:38). Auras also has "Rows" (max rows), which nobody else has.
  • Spacing key: spacing (AB, Auras) vs gap (Cooldowns Options.lua:71).
  • Visibility: AB uses switches "Fade when idle"/"Show on mouseover" (Options.lua:209-232). DataBars uses "Only on mouseover"/"Hide in combat" (Options.lua:108-109). Cooldowns uses a dropdown labelled only "Show" (Options.lua:90, options at 46-51). Auras has none.
  • Reload handling: AB calls ns:RequestReload (Options.lua:124,273,311). Cooldowns/DataBars/Auras just print "Type /rl to apply this." (Cooldowns:19-21, DataBars:12-14, Auras:11-13).
  • Names: DataBars page is titled "Info bars" (DataBars/Options.lua:218), its look entry is "Data bars" (Options.lua:305, General.lua:279), and its sub is "Data text bars".
  • Where look rows land is inconsistent. AB gets its own "Borders and corners" sub-page. Cooldowns and Auras get them on "General". DataBars gets them on "Data text bars", although corners and texture also drive the Experience/Reputation/Honor bars. The Cooldowns bar texture lands on General > General tab (General.lua:278), not on the Buff bars sub-page that uses it.
  • Mover sub-page targets: action bars, stance and pet movers pass page = "actionbars" with no sub (Bars.lua:333, Special.lua:199,204). Micro, eye and the other modules pass a sub (Micro.lua:329, Eye.lua:64, Manager.lua:399, Auras.lua:292).
  • Spec filtering is fragile. Some filters match translated labels: AB SPECIAL_SKIP (Options.lua:247), Cooldowns MOVER_LABELS (Options.lua:126-129), DataBars TEXT_BAR_MOVER (Options.lua:113-116). Others pick by index: Auras (Options.lua:48-51), DataBars D.MoverSpecs (Options.lua:205-208). Reordering rows or editing a locale string silently changes what the movers show.
  • Cooldowns "Number size" (%, global, Countdown numbers tab) vs "Countdown size" (px, per viewer, Look tab) (Options.lua:191 vs 119): two different sizes for the same numbers with near-identical labels.
  • DataBars stores text size under the key font (Options.lua:93, Panels.lua:34,599).
  • Stance/Pet "Buttons per row" slider goes to 12 while SLOTS = 10 (Options.lua:155 vs Special.lua:7,121).
  • D.MAX_SLOTS is never defined. Options.lua:42 falls back to 12, which duplicates local MAX_SLOTS = 12 in Panels.lua:8.

3.4 Defaults never read, and keys read but missing from defaults

grep found no default that is never read. Every key in the four AddDefaults tables is read somewhere. Defaults that are read but have no UI row (dead toggles from the user's side): - cooldowns.skinManager (Data.lua:10; read Cooldowns.lua:112,143) - cooldowns.flatBars (Data.lua:12; read Cooldowns.lua:94) - cooldowns.manager.bars.flat (Data.lua:20; read Manager.lua:149, Preview.lua:68) - databars.textBarsSeeded, textBarsStretched (migration flags, Panels.lua:701-709), databars.volumeChannel (set by clicking the volume text)

Keys read or written but missing from defaults: actionbars.leaveSize (Extra.lua:131,157 or 32), actionbars.matchUndo (Bars.lua:287), databars.barTexture (Bars.lua:377), cooldowns.manager.bars.barTexture (Manager.lua:149). The Cooldowns texture dropdown getter returns nil (Options.lua:29 ViewerSpec), so it shows no selection until the user picks one; W.BarTextureSpec handles this with or "default" (BarTexture.lua:86). The per-text-bar defaults are in Panels.lua:26-43, not Data.lua. icons defaults to "lixard" there, but the options getter falls back to "addon" (DataBars/Options.lua:101), so old bars without the key show "addon".

3.5 Rows with no desc

  • ActionBars: "New rows go" (Options.lua:169-178), on 10 bars.
  • Cooldowns: Width (70), Spacing (71), Icons per row (74), Grow (75), New rows go (80), Center each row (85), Show (90), Icon zoom (112), Space after icon (115), Text size (116), Stack size (117), Countdown size (119), Charges/Stack size (120), Charges/Stack position (121), Number size (191), Corners (214). Expanded, that is 43 of the 64 Cooldowns rows.
  • Auras/AuraHide: "Longer than (minutes)" (AuraHide.lua:156-163). "Hidden spells" has descText only (AuraHide.lua:172-179).
  • DataBars: none missing.

4. Position and size: where stored, and how it relates to the mover

Position. One store for all four modules: ns.DB.profile.layouts[<layoutName>][<moverKey>], normally layouts.Default (SHARED_LAYOUT = "Default", Layout.lua:13; LayoutName() Layout.lua:24-38; default {} at Layout.lua:15). Store() (Layout.lua:297-311) saves either { point, x, y } or an anchor link Link(mover, anchor, side, x, y). Unsaved frames use the default passed to Layout:Register. Layout tool prefs are in G.layoutTools (Layout.lua:16).

Mover keys: - ActionBars: actionbar_bar1..8 (Bars.lua:331, default DefaultPosition, Bars.lua:192-204), stanceBar (anchored to actionbar_bar5, Special.lua:194-200), petBar (Special.lua:202), microMenu, bagsBar (Micro.lua:327), queueEye (Eye.lua:60), vehicleLeave (Extra.lua:149) - Cooldowns: cdmEssential, cdmUtility, cdmBuffs (the last two pinned to essential), cdmBars (Manager.lua:10-16, 397) - DataBars: xpBar, repBar, honorBar (Bars.lua:451), dataText<id> e.g. dataTextbar1 (Panels.lua:639) - Auras: playerBuffs (pinned to minimap), playerDebuffs (pinned to playerBuffs) (Auras.lua:18-19, 287-293)

Size. The mover never stores size. Layout has no drag-resize; the only "resizing" is a guard flag (Layout.lua:987-993) that re-applies the saved spot when the frame's own size changes. All sizes live in the module tables: actionbars.bars.*.{size,spacing,buttons,perRow}, actionbars.{microScale,bagsBarScale,eyeScale,leaveSize}, cooldowns.manager.*.{width,height,gap,perRow}, databars.{xp,rep,honor}.{width,height}, databars.textBars.*.{width,height,slots,stretch}, auras.*.{size,perRow,rows,spacing}.

Options rows that also write position: - AB "Match Blizzard's layout" (Layout:Move, Bars.lua:308-314), "Undo Match" (Layout:Move/Reset, Bars.lua:255-260), "Default bar layout" (Layout:Reset, Bars.lua:275-278). Undo state is in actionbars.matchUndo, which holds both sizes and x/y (Bars.lua:221-234). - Cooldowns "Stack the bars" (Layout:Stack, Manager.lua:409-412) rewrites cdmUtility/cdmBuffs as anchor links. - DataBars "Delete this bar" calls Layout:Unregister, which deletes the saved spot (Panels.lua:673, Layout.lua:1014-1026). "Stretch across the screen" limits the mover to vertical moves (Panels.lua:576). - /lui micro reset|center moves or resets microMenu/bagsBar (Micro.lua:506-511).

How it fits together. Each mover's gear opens Dialog.lua's SettingsPanel (Dialog.lua:102-120). It renders options.settings() with Builder.Row, using the same spec closures as the options page, so values stay in sync. The dialog is the only place for the four AB scale/size keys (microScale, bagsBarScale, eyeScale, leaveSize), and it shows advanced rows unfiltered. Its "All settings" jump uses options.page (Dialog.lua:43-46), which for action/stance/pet bars has no sub-page. Widgets/Positions.lua (anchor table for placing text and icons inside a frame) and Widgets/PlaceEditor.lua (drag-to-place elements) are not used by any of these four modules (grep). They only serve unit/group frames, so they play no part in these frames' position or size.

Part 4

Settings audit: LixardUI_Tracker and LixardUI_Bags

Read-only audit. Paths relative to LixardUI.

How rows reach the screen (this decides where each row ends up):

  • The module's Options.lua registers its page with ns:AddOptionsPage (LixardUI/Core/Entry.lua:25). retailOnly rows are dropped on Forever (Entry.lua:15-23).
  • ns:AddLookSpecs(title, specs, sub) only queues rows (Entry.lua:11). When the settings addon loads, LixardUI_Options/Pages/General.lua:314-324 runs MoveHome. HOMES sends "Objective tracker" to page tracker, sub Look, and "Bags" to page bags, sub Look (General.lua:272-293). Each queued group becomes a new section named after its kind (Fonts / Borders and corners / Backgrounds). "Mythic+ timer" has no HOMES entry, so it stays on General > Bars.
  • Next, LixardUI_Options/Pages/Extra.lua:31-48 moves any section with home= onto another page, if that page is registered: tracker Mythic+ goes to QoL, bags Selling goes to Vendor, bags Open bags/Closing go to QoL. skipIf rows are removed first (Extra.lua:19-29).
  • advanced = true rows are hidden until "Show advanced" is on (LixardUI_Options/Options.lua:581-630).

Tracker load order (LixardUI_Tracker.toc): Data, Groups, Content, Auto, Feedback, Party, Tooltip, Items, Menu, Keystone, Window, Probe, Options, Init. Bags load order (LixardUI_Bags.toc): Data, Look, Buttons, BagBar, Categories, Sort, Junk, Search, Window, Bank, Alts, AltView, Hooks, AutoOpen, Probe, Options, Init.

SV roots: - T:DB() = ns.DB.profile.tracker (LixardUI_Tracker/Data.lua:79-81) - B:DB() = ns.DB.profile.bags (LixardUI_Bags/Data.lua:55-57) - borders = ns.DB.profile.moduleBorders.<module> (LixardUI/Theme/Theme.lua:325-332)

3. Problems found

3a. Look settings repeated per module

  • Corners: three controls write the same keys.
  • Tracker "Corners" writes tracker.corners (LixardUI_Tracker/Options.lua:592-602).
  • Bags "Item shape" writes bags.shape (LixardUI_Bags/Options.lua:45-54).
  • General > Borders and corners > "Corners" overwrites both through Theme:SetCornersEverywhere (LixardUI/Theme/Theme.lua:338-341, 350-355; row at LixardUI_Options/Pages/General.lua:225-237).
  • The bags row uses a different label ("Item shape") and key name (shape) for the same value set.
  • Seven modules repeat a "Corners" row: Auras, Cooldowns, DataBars, GroupFrames, Tooltips, Tracker, UnitFrames.
  • Border color: 14 modules call BorderSpecs("<module>") (each adds 2 rows). Tracker is at LixardUI_Tracker/Options.lua:4,620 and Bags at LixardUI_Bags/Options.lua:4,583. General already has the global pair plus "Apply to all", which resets every module's mode (Theme.lua SetBorderEverywhere, General.lua:240-250). The "Border color picker" row has no disabled when mode is not custom, and setting it silently flips mode to custom (LixardUI/Widgets/BorderSpecs.lua:51-55).
  • Text size is three different concepts with one label.
  • General "Text size" = fonts.scale, a multiplier (General.lua:105-112).
  • Bags "Text size" = an absolute size for ilvl and BoE (LixardUI_Bags/Options.lua:110-116), but ilvl is drawn at textSize+2 and BoE at textSize-1 (LixardUI_Bags/Look.lua:139-140,189-191), so the slider value is never the size shown. Its desc "Size of the item level and BoE text" is therefore misleading.
  • Tracker has two absolute sizes, "Quest name size" and "Objective size" (LixardUI_Tracker/Options.lua:555-569), and they sit in General > Layout, not under Look.
  • "Text size" appears in 6 module Options files (Bags, Cooldowns, DataBars, GroupFrames, Meters, UnitFrames).
  • Text outline: per-module copy. Tracker outline (LixardUI_Tracker/Options.lua:604-616) is the only tracker row in the Fonts group, is advanced, and leaves an advanced-only "Fonts" section. Chat repeats it. There is no global outline. The font face comes from General (fonts.body/heading), but outline and size are per module.
  • Bar texture. The tracker Mythic+ timer row is registered via AddLookSpecs with title "Mythic+ timer", which has no HOMES entry, so it lands on General > Bars, not on the tracker or QoL Mythic+ page where the timer's other settings are (LixardUI_Tracker/Options.lua:622, General.lua:272-293). tracker.barTexture has no default in Data.lua and relies on or "default" (LixardUI/Widgets/BarTexture.lua:86).
  • Scale: different units.
  • Bags scale is a 0.6-1.5 fraction shown as % (LixardUI_Bags/Options.lua:515-521).
  • Tracker keyScale is a 60-160 integer % (LixardUI_Tracker/Options.lua:294-300).
  • The quest tracker has no scale at all.
  • Four modules have their own "Scale".
  • Background. Tracker background switch (LixardUI_Tracker/Options.lua:584-590). Bags uses a different concept for the same job, "Smoked glass background" (LixardUI_Bags/Options.lua:531-541). DataBars and Meters repeat "Background".
  • Spacing wording. "Space between quests" (Tracker o:503) vs "Spacing" (Bags o:508); 5 modules have "Spacing".

3b. Same SV key controlled from two (or more) places

key places
tracker.corners Tracker Look (o:592), General Corners (Theme.lua:339,350-355)
bags.shape Bags Item shape (o:45), General Corners (Theme.lua:341)
moduleBorders.tracker/bags.mode module row, General "Border color everywhere" (Theme.lua SetBorderEverywhere)
tracker.sortBy, finished, groupZones, currentOnly, watchAll Options rows and the tracker sort menu (LixardUI_Tracker/Menu.lua:115-122) — documented in desc, OK
bags.sortMode/sortBy Options (o:191-213) and the bag Sort right-click menu (LixardUI_Bags/Window.lua:303-315)
bags.bankSortMode/bankSortBy Options (o:406-427) and the bank sort menu (LixardUI_Bags/Bank.lua:659-671)
bags.bankView Options (o:392-404) and the bank view menu (Bank.lua:708-716)
bags.view, showBagBar Options and window buttons (Window.lua:426,436)
bags.columns / bankColumns slider and resize grip (Window.lua:466, Bank.lua:929)
bags.autoSell vs vendor.autoSell.enabled Two switches for one behaviour. The bags row is hidden when Vendor is loaded (o:260); Vendor copies it once (LixardUI_Vendor/Sell.lua:148-154); Junk.lua:141 sells only without Vendor. If the user turns Vendor off later, the old bags.autoSell value comes back.
bags.junkMarks / neverSell Cleared from the Bags page; read by Vendor (LixardUI_Vendor/Sell.lua:26-37)
tracker.keyOnly vs tracker.rules.challenge Two settings that both hide the tracker in keys: "Hide quests in keys" (o:302, used in Window.lua:856) and "Mythic+ keys" place rule (Auto.lua:23, o:47-60). They end up on different pages once Mythic+ moves to QoL.
tracker.replace / bags.replaceBank vs Modules page The Modules page module switch (LixardUI_Options/Pages/Modules.lua:27-41) plus a second in-module "Use the LixardUI tracker/bank". The two reload paths differ: tracker prints "Type /rl" (o:19-26), bags calls ns:RequestReload (o:389).
tracker.maxHeight → layout position The Max height slider moves the saved mover position (LixardUI_Tracker/Window.lua:1097-1103); see 4

No other module, LixardUI core file or LixardUI_Options page writes profile.tracker.* or profile.bags.* directly. Only Theme.lua (corners) and Vendor (reads, plus the one-time autoSell copy) touch them; Mail reads global.bagsAlts.chars (LixardUI_Mail/Contacts.lua:54).

3c. Inconsistent widgets, wording, behaviour

  • Bank sort choices are hard-coded in LixardUI_Bags/Options.lua:409-416, duplicating B.SORT_BY (Window.lua:294-300), which the bag Sort row uses (o:196). The bank view choices (o:396-401) duplicate VIEWS in Bank.lua. Bank.lua:663 already uses B.SORT_BY.
  • Missing LIXARDUI_SETTINGS_CHANGED sends, so an open options window or mover dialog does not refresh (LixardUI/Layout/Dialog.lua:196, LixardUI_Options/Options.lua:1593). Affected:
  • Tracker: the 5 sound setters (o:334-381).
  • Bags: Sort by, sortStack, junkLast, sortReverse, autoSell, all openWith/closeWith, bankView, bankSort, bankColumns, tooltipCounts (o:204-234, 263, 304-374, 403, 418-434, 463).
  • Bags Setter("scale") only calls B.Place() for the bag window (o:10, 521). The bank's scale is applied only in PlaceBank (Bank.lua:191), so the bank does not rescale live (likely, not verified in game).
  • Tracker "Objective size" refreshes with T.Refresh while "Quest name size" uses Relayout (o:560 vs o:568).
  • The dependent row comes before its switch: "Progress line size" (o:384) is listed above "Progress line on screen" (o:393) and has no disabled guard. Mythic+ width/height/scale have no disabled = not keyBlock, while "Hide quests in keys" does (o:307).
  • The "Check this game" probe button sits under General > Layout (o:571), not near Troubleshooting.
  • Bags "Open bags" is both a sub-page name (o:296, 365) and a button label (o:562). "Hidden bank tabs" and "Bank position" are noun labels on action buttons; other buttons use verb labels ("Reset sections", "Unlock all slots").
  • "Forget a character" is a dropdown used as an action button, with get always "" (o:466-483).
  • The Tracker page description says "Move it in Edit Mode or with /lui move" (o:67), but the bag and bank windows are not movers at all (see 4). The Bags page says to drag, which is inconsistent across the two modules.
  • "Show keyring" is inserted only if B.HasKeyring() is true when Options.lua loads (o:573). The keyring size may not be known yet at load, so the row can be missing.
  • The tracker border row's section is titled "Objective tracker", but the setting also restyles the Mythic+ timer and quest item buttons (Keystone.lua:501, Items.lua:69 use "frame:tracker").

3d. Defaults never read

Every Data.lua key was grepped across both modules, excluding Options.lua. All are read somewhere at runtime, so there are no dead keys. Edge cases:

  • bags.bankOneBag (LixardUI_Bags/Data.lua:44) is never written. It is only a legacy fallback for bankView (Bank.lua:94, Options o:402), so in effect it is the hidden default for bankView ("one").
  • bags.showQuality (Data.lua:13) is redundant with qualityStyle = "off". The Options row writes both (o:69-71).
  • Keys used with no Data.lua default:
  • tracker: barTexture
  • bags: bankView, bankSortMode, bankSortBy, point, bankPoint
  • All rely on inline or fallbacks.

3e. Rows with no desc

  • LixardUI_Tracker/Options.lua:276-282 Mythic+ timer width
  • LixardUI_Tracker/Options.lua:293-300 Mythic+ timer scale
  • LixardUI_Bags/Options.lua:301-360, all 10 "Open bags with" switches

4. Position and size storage, and the mover

Tracker (registered with the mover): - Quest tracker: ns.Layout:Register(anchor, "tracker", ...) (LixardUI_Tracker/Window.lua:1240-1248). Position: ns.DB.profile.layouts[<layout>].tracker = { point, x, y }, or a link {anchor, side, ...} (LixardUI/Layout/Layout.lua:40-49,297-322; layout name "Default" or the largest saved, Layout.lua:13,24-38). Default: below the minimap, TOPRIGHT, y -8, fallback TOPRIGHT -4,-240. - Size is not in layouts. It lives in profile.tracker.width / maxHeight and is applied to the anchor (Window.lua:1216,1095). - Mythic+ timer: mover key keystone (Keystone.lua:507-519). Position: layouts[..].keystone. Size: tracker.keyWidth/keyHeight/keyScale (Keystone.lua:480-483). Its default spot is the same as the tracker's (below minimap, TOPRIGHT, y -8), so the two overlap by default whenever keyOnly is off. - Progress line: mover key trackerAlert (Feedback.lua:82-85). Position: layouts[..].trackerAlert, default 0,220. Size comes from tracker.progressSize (Feedback.lua:89-95). - An options row also writes position. Changing Max height runs T.Relayout, which calls ns.Layout:Move(mover, x, y - delta/2) (Window.lua:1097-1103). That goes through Store, so it saves the layout spot and logs it (Layout.lua:297-322,377). The top edge stays fixed, but the saved layout entry is rewritten from a settings slider. If the tracker is linked to another frame, Store keeps the link (Layout.lua:309-311). - Mover gear popup: settings = W.PickSpecs(T.Page, {Width, Max height, Style, Background, ...}) (Window.lua:1244-1246). Same specs objects, so the same keys. - "Background" is only in T.Page after General.lua's MoveHome has run, which happens when the settings addon loads. - LixardUI/Layout/Dialog.lua:105-106 calls settings() before ns.EnsureOptions() and caches the panel per mover (dialog.panels[mover]). If the gear is opened before the settings window has ever been opened, the tracker popup lacks Background for the rest of the session. - Keystone's popup falls back to the QoL page after Extra.lua moves the section (Keystone.lua:511-516). With the same ordering it gets T.Page's copies first, which is fine. - LixardUI/Widgets/Positions.lua and PlaceEditor.lua are not used by Tracker or Bags. They handle element placement inside frames (unit-frame text etc.); grep finds no references in either module.

Bags (not registered with the mover): - The bag window is moved by dragging its title. SavePosition writes profile.bags.point = { point, relativePoint, x, y } relative to UIParent (LixardUI_Bags/Window.lua:23-27,401). Default BOTTOMRIGHT -60,120 (Window.lua:29-39). - The bank uses profile.bags.bankPoint (Bank.lua:176-192), default TOPLEFT 40,-120. - Size is columns × (size + spacing), from bags.columns/bankColumns, size, spacing, plus bags.scale (both windows). - The resize grip writes columns/bankColumns and re-anchors the window to TOPLEFT and saves the point (Window.lua:355-361,466; Bank.lua:929). - Options rows that write position: "Window position" Reset sets bags.point = nil (o:550-559); "Bank position" Reset sets bankPoint = nil (o:445-451). - Because these windows are outside ns.Layout, they do not appear in /lui move or Edit Mode, have no snapping, and are not part of profile.layouts. Layout presets or "Default" layout sharing therefore never carry bag or bank positions. Their positions are per profile in the bags table instead.

Part 5

Settings audit: LixardUI_Minimap and LixardUI_QoL

Read-only audit, 2026-11-10. Paths are relative to LixardUI.

How the pages are put together (needed to read the tables):

  • Minimap .toc order: Data, Frame, Texts, Buttons, Difficulty, Drawer, Extras, North, Probe, Options, Init. Options.lua runs after Buttons.lua, so M.BUTTONS exists when the Map buttons spec loop runs (LixardUI_Minimap/Options.lua:155-264).
  • QoL .toc order: Data, Move, MacroSearch, ExpansionFilter, Portals, DungeonReady, TokenAlert, Cursor, BuffReminder, LoadoutReminder, TalentPanel, TalkingHead, FastLoot, Keystone, Summon, BattleRes, CraftOrders, ClickMenu, GroupTools, MyRole, AddonData, AddonWindow, Options, Init.
  • ns:AddLookSpecs (LixardUI/Core/Entry.lua:11) queues look rows. LixardUI_Options/Pages/General.lua:295-325 (MoveHome, HOMES at :283 [Minimap] = { "minimap", "Map" }) then appends them to the minimap page's Map sub-page as new sections: one titled Fonts (from the "fonts" group) and one titled Borders and corners (default group). They do not stay on General.
  • section.home / homeSub (LixardUI_Options/Pages/Extra.lua:31-48) moves sections between pages. QoL sends 2 sections to the Auction page; Bags, Tracker and Friends send 4 sections into the QoL page.
  • subOrder (LixardUI_QoL/Options.lua:38) sorts QoL sub-pages (Extra.lua:50-62).
  • retailOnly / foreverOnly filters are applied in Entry.lua:15-32.
  • Advanced rows: on a page with sub-pages, every advanced = true row is moved into one extra "Advanced" card per sub-page (LixardUI_Options/Options.lua:598-635). QoL has no advanced rows.
  • Mover popups (LixardUI/Layout/Dialog.lua:103-118) show options.settings() rows. Most of these are the same spec objects as the page rows, picked by label with W.PickSpecs (LixardUI/Widgets/BorderSpecs.lua:60-74).

SV roots: ns.DB.profile.minimap (M:DB(), Minimap/Data.lua:60-62), ns.DB.profile.qol (Q:DB(), QoL/Data.lua:59-61), ns.DB.global.addonManager (QoL/AddonData.lua:9-17,28-30), ns.DB.char.myRole (QoL/MyRole.lua:6), ns.DB.char.loadoutRules/loadoutNames (QoL/LoadoutReminder.lua:7-8), ns.DB.profile.moduleBorders.minimap (LixardUI/Theme/Theme.lua:325-332), ns.DB.profile.layouts.Default.<moverKey> (LixardUI/Layout/Layout.lua:13-48).

1. Row tables
