3. Problems found

Look settings that repeat across modules

  • P1. The minimap has two separate border-color systems on one card, with different choices. Map border color uses minimap.borderColor with black/theme/accent/class/custom and its own RGBA picker (Minimap/Options.lua:456-485, Frame.lua:72-89). Right below it, the shared BorderSpecs pair (Options.lua:4, Widgets/BorderSpecs.lua:7-58) uses moduleBorders.minimap with default/theme/custom/class/none. "Accent" exists only in the first, "None" only in the second, and "Custom" vs "Color" name the same thing. That means 4 color rows and 2 pickers labelled "Map border color picker" and "Border color picker" (Options.lua:473, BorderSpecs.lua:45).
  • P2. Map border color "Theme" is really the Container border color. The fallback branch returns Theme:Color("frame:minimap") (Frame.lua:87-88), which resolves through FrameColor("minimap") to moduleBorders.minimap (Theme.lua:46-66,116-118). So setting Container border color to "No border" makes the map ring invisible (alpha 0) when Map border color = Theme.
  • P3. "Container border color" says it is only for the addon container, but it also colors every flat map button and the drawer toggle. The desc (Options.lua:4) says "The border around the right-click addon container", but the color is applied by frame:minimap on Buttons.lua:173, 476 and Drawer.lua:102, 239, 482, 516.
  • P4. Minimap text look is its own per-module set and doesn't use the shared TextStyle. It has three separate size sliders (zoneSize/clockSize/coordsSize, Options.lua:70-76,93-99,133-139) plus a module outline switch (textOutline, Options.lua:487-494), and no font, shadow or color choice. Compare ns.TextStyles:Specs (Widgets/TextStyle.lua:143-224, used by GroupFrames) and TS:GlobalSpecs (TextStyle.lua:226-241). The same "outline" pattern repeats per module (Tracker OutlineSpec Tracker/Options.lua:618, Chat Chat/Options.lua:233).
  • P5. Corner/shape is repeated. Minimap has its own shape and corner (Options.lua:28-38, 432-445). Elsewhere the convention is <module>.corners (Theme.lua:338-342 CORNER_FIELDS) with CornerSpec in ActionBars, Auras, Cooldowns, DataBars, Tooltips, Tracker and UnitFrames. minimap.corner isn't in CORNER_FIELDS, and the Minimap choices (Small/Medium/Large/Very large) don't match the General Corners choices (Clipped/Square, General.lua:229-231).
  • P6. A third color-mode set: the cursor (QoL/Cursor.lua:20-22: class/accent/custom, labels "Class color/Accent color/Pick a color") plus a separate picker (QoL/Options.lua:217-231). So there are three color-mode vocabularies across these two modules.
  • P7. Fade/alpha settings repeat across modules with different keys and ranges.
  • Minimap fadeAlpha 10-100 and combatRule/combatAlpha (Minimap/Data.lua:52-54).
  • Tracker fadeAlpha and combatRule with the same label "Fade until hovered" and different rule values (Tracker/Data.lua:45-46, Tracker/Options.lua:40,254).
  • Maps fadeAlpha and combatFade/combatAlpha (Maps/Data.lua:12,23-24).
  • UnitFrames fadeAlpha (UnitFrames/Data.lua:66).
  • Percent format is inconsistent: Minimap fade sliders show a bare number (Options.lua:342-365), while QoL Opacity shows % (QoL/Options.lua:204).
  • P8. Size vs scale wording is mixed.
  • "Size" is pixels for the map (Options.lua:42).
  • "Scale" is a raw multiplier 0.5-2 (Options.lua:50).
  • "%s size" is pixels for mail (Options.lua:208) but a scale shown as % for landing (Options.lua:235-240).
  • The difficulty "Size" is a scale shown as % (Difficulty.lua:57-60).
  • Tracker "Mythic+ timer scale" 60-160 % is a different unit again (Tracker/Options.lua:293-297).

Defaults never read

  • P9. minimap.buttons.difficulty (Minimap/Data.lua:36) and minimap.buttons.queue (Data.lua:37) are never read. Mode()/Settings() (Buttons.lua:134-142) are only called with entry.key from M.BUTTONS (Buttons.lua:145,290,295,308,314,341,395,409,438), and that list has neither key (Buttons.lua:101-109). Difficulty has its own holder (difficultyScale, instanceDifficulty mover). Grep found no other buttons.difficulty or buttons.queue use.
  • All other keys in minimap, qol, addonManager, myRole, loadoutRules and loadoutNames are read somewhere (grep results cited in the tables). The zone/clock/coords Pos and Size keys are read by name concatenation (Texts.lua:37-43), so a plain grep misses them.
  • P10. Keys used without a default: minimap.rotate (Extras.lua:109,114) and qol.cursorCustom (QoL/Options.lua:222,226; Cursor.lua:34). Both work through nil fallbacks, but they are missing from Data.lua.

Same SV key controlled from two places

  • P11. minimap.shape is also written by General > Borders and corners > Corners (General.lua:227-237 → Theme:SetCornersEverywhere, Theme.lua:357-360). It flips shape to "square"/"rounded" whenever it isn't "round", and it never touches minimap.corner. That row's desc says "each can still pick its own below" (General.lua:228), but the Minimap rows were moved to the Minimap page (General.lua:283,295-311).
  • P12. moduleBorders.minimap.mode is reset by General's "Border color everywhere" (Theme.lua:371-375), but minimap.borderColor is not. So "everywhere" leaves the map ring alone (unless it is on Theme, see P2).
  • minimap.drawerMode is set from the options dropdown (Options.lua:270-280) and from the drawer button's right-click menu (Drawer.lua:429-431). Both go through SetMode (Drawer.lua:375-381), so this is consistent and intended. drawerKeep and drawerOrder are menu-only.
  • minimap.rotate and the CVar rotateMinimap. The option writes the CVar (Extras.lua:108-111). KeepRotate (Extras.lua:119-123) forces it back whenever it differs, so Blizzard's own Rotate Minimap setting gets overridden. This may also go against the AGENTS.md rule "Never write shared CVars". I'm not sure whether rotateMinimap is account-wide, so please check.
  • Talking head can be hidden from two places with two keys: qol.hideTalkingHead (QoL/Options.lua:476-486, TalkingHead.lua:6) and the Blizzard mover popup "Hide it" → profile.blizzardMovers.talkingHead.hide (Layout/Blizzard.lua:23,33-37,120-131).
  • The mover popups reuse the same row objects (same key, second UI; fine by design): Minimap map (Frame.lua:199-201), free map buttons (Buttons.lua:366), buff reminder Show and Icon size (BuffReminder.lua:808), battle res (BattleRes.lua:96), group tools (GroupTools.lua:334), and the Tracker key timer width/height/scale (Tracker/Keystone.lua:511-515).
  • Searches for profile.minimap, profile.qol, .qol., myRole and addonManager in LixardUI core, LixardUI_Options and the other modules found only Theme.lua:357 (P11). No other module writes these keys.

Broken or inconsistent wiring

  • P13. The Minimap mover popup asks for labels that don't exist. Frame.lua:200 asks PickSpecs for "Border thickness" and "Border color", but the real labels are "Map border thickness" (Options.lua:449), "Map border color" (:458) and "Container border color" (:4). Those two rows silently drop out of the popup.
  • The free-button mover popup shows the wrong control for mail and landing. It always offers "Button size" (Buttons.lua:366). Mail uses buttons.mail.size, which defaults to 30 (Data.lua:33), so ButtonSize never falls back to buttonSize (Buttons.lua:144-146,541). Landing uses scale (Buttons.lua:341). For those two the popup slider does nothing.
  • P14. Some setters don't send LIXARDUI_SETTINGS_CHANGED, unlike their neighbours:
  • mail size (Minimap/Options.lua:212-215)
  • landing scale/angle/push ButtonSetter (Options.lua:226-231)
  • Rotate (Options.lua:379)
  • difficulty mover-popup Size (Difficulty.lua:62-65)
  • addon manager replaceBlizzard (QoL/Options.lua:518)
  • all BorderSpecs setters (BorderSpecs.lua:34-56)
  • P15. Map buttons rows depend on load timing. entry.frame() is checked once when Options.lua loads (Options.lua:178), so a frame created later never gets rows. Landing is listed even where the frame doesn't exist, because adjust short-circuits the check.
  • P16. Uneven disabled-state handling.
  • "Combat fade" isn't disabled when In combat ≠ "fade" (Options.lua:358-365), though its desc says it only applies then.
  • "Cursor color" isn't disabled when the cursor is off, while its four neighbours are (QoL/Options.lua:217-231 vs :198,207,215,237).
  • "Show my loadout on ready checks" is a sibling of the reminder switch but doesn't depend on it (fine, but it sits in the same section).
  • P17. The QoL subOrder and nav have stale entries.
  • subOrder lists "Selling and repair", "Mail" and "Alerts" (QoL/Options.lua:38), but no section anywhere uses those sub names (grep: "Alerts" only appears in Options.lua:81 and QoL Options.lua:38).
  • The nav chapter { "qol", L["Alerts"] } (LixardUI_Options/Options.lua:81) points at a sub-page that doesn't exist.
  • { "qol", L["Auctions and crafting"] } (LixardUI_Options/Options.lua:74) is empty whenever the Auction page exists, because both QoL sections in that sub have home = "auction" (QoL/Options.lua:72-73,373-374).
  • P18. A generic label is used for a PickSpecs lookup. Buff reminder's dropdown is labelled just "Show" (QoL/Options.lua:141), and BuffReminder.lua:808 finds it with PickSpecs on the whole QoL page by that label. It works only because no other "Show" row is on the page today. The label alone also doesn't say what it shows.

Rows with no desc

  • Minimap: "%s size" for landing (Minimap/Options.lua:233-240).
  • QoL:
  • Test the alert (:113-119)
  • Food, Flask or phial, Weapon oil or stone, Augment rune (:148-151, ReminderCheck called with no desc)
  • Warn when less than (minutes) (:154-161)
  • Icon size (:162-169)
  • Ring size (:192-199), Opacity (:200-208), Color (:209-216), Only in combat (:232-238)
  • Prices from (:448-459)
  • When you enter, On ready check, Test the reminder (LoadoutReminder.lua:309-336)
  • Foreign rows on QoL: Mythic+ timer width and scale (Tracker/Options.lua:276-300), and all 10 Open bags with switches (Bags/Options.lua:294-358, not individually checked).

Wording inconsistencies

  • Move hints vary: "Move it in Edit Mode or with /lui move" (Minimap/Options.lua:22); "Move it with /lui move" (QoL :99,135,256,263,276,363); "Anywhere (move it with /lui move)" (Buttons.lua:30).
  • "Group full popup" sits under the Mythic+ sub-page, but its desc covers any Group Finder dungeon group (QoL/Options.lua:255-256).
  • "Icon search in the macro window" sits under Play helpers (QoL/Options.lua:489-499), though it is a window helper.
  • Section and row share a title: "WoW Token alert" (section :92, row :98), "Faster loot" (:309, :314), "Buff reminder" (:129, :134) and "Talent loadout reminder" (:123, LoadoutReminder.lua:304). This repeats the label in the card header.
  • The toggle style for "on/off" checks varies. The Minimap uses dropdowns with an "off" value for text places (TEXT_PLACES includes "Off", Texts.lua:18) and for the cursor ("Off", Cursor.lua:13), while other features use a master switch plus disabled.

QoL is a grab-bag: where each row really belongs

QoL row(s) Better owner
Dungeon teleports, Group full popup, Show who has the key, Insert keystone, Start on the countdown Mythic+ feature area, with Tracker's Mythic+ timer (already homed in) and Friends Keystones (already homed in). Group full popup is really Group Finder.
Talent loadout reminder (6 rows) Talent features (TalentPanel.lua / LoadoutReminder.lua are their own subsystem; see the LixardUI talent features plan)
Buff reminder (11 rows) Auras (buffs) or a "Readiness" module
Group tools button, Hide Blizzard's raid tools, My role, Summon countdown, Battle res tracker GroupFrames (party/raid)
Auto expansion filter x2, Crafting orders x10 Auction (already moved there by home)

WoW Token alert DataBars info texts / alerts
Cursor finder (6 rows) General > Look (on-screen look)
Faster loot Bags (loot / items)

Icon search in the macro window Window helpers (Skins)
Addon manager, replace Blizzard AddOns button System / own page (already its own sub-page)

The page then also carries Bags (11 rows), Tracker (6) and Friends (2) rows, so on retail 63 rows come from 4 modules.

4. Position and size storage vs options rows vs mover

Mover storage. Layout:Register(frame, key, ...) (Layout/Layout.lua:976-1001) saves to ns.DB.profile.layouts["Default"][key]. The layout name is SHARED_LAYOUT = "Default" (Layout.lua:13), or the largest existing layout (:24-38). The value is { point, x, y } (:311) or a link { anchor, side, ... } (:264-280, 308, 340). Reset sets it to nil (:381-385). The mover dialog (Layout/Dialog.lua) has x/y fields, Reset position, "All settings" (jumps to options.page) and the options.settings() rows.

Frame Mover key → profile.layouts.Default.<key> Default anchor Size/scale stored in Options rows write position? Options rows write size?
Minimap holder minimap (Frame.lua:195-203) to dataTextbar1 TOPRIGHT→BOTTOMRIGHT y-4, fallback TOPRIGHT -4,-4 minimap.size, minimap.scale, minimap.borderSize (inset adds to holder size, Frame.lua:126-127) No Yes. Size, Scale and Map border thickness all resize the holder. Layout re-applies through the OnSizeChanged hook (Layout.lua:987-993). The same rows are in the mover popup (Frame.lua:200), minus the two broken labels (P13).
Free map buttons minimapButton_<entry.key> (Buttons.lua:356-370), created only when a button's spot = FREE UIParent centre -240,-60 buttonSize / buttons.mail.size / buttons.landing.scale Indirectly. The "%s spot" dropdown (buttons.<k>.at, Options.lua:191-204) decides whether the button is map-anchored (spot/ANGLE plus angle/push) or mover-owned (FREE). The angle and distance sliders are a second positioning system for landing. Yes (button size rows); the popup shows the wrong one for mail/landing (P13)
Difficulty icon instanceDifficulty (Difficulty.lua:92-97) to minimap BOTTOMLEFT 4,4 minimap.difficultyScale No Mover popup only; no settings page row
Drawer / bar not a mover; placed by drawerSide relative to the map or cursor (Drawer.lua:298-335) - drawerSize, drawerColumns Yes. Drawer side is its own placement dropdown. Yes
Zone / clock / coords texts not movers; placed by zonePos/clockPos/coordsPos (Texts.lua:37-39) using their own M.TEXT_PLACES list (Texts.lua:13-19), not the shared W.POSITIONS / W.POSITION_OPTIONS (Widgets/Positions.lua:6-56) - *Size Yes, text place dropdowns Yes
Buff reminder buffReminder (BuffReminder.lua:807-810) 0, h/2-220 qol.reminderSize No Yes. Icon size is also in the mover popup.
Battle res battleRes (BattleRes.lua:95-98) w/2-300, 200 fixed No No
Group tools button groupTools (GroupTools.lua:333-336) w/2-340, 160 fixed No No
Group full popup dungeonReady (DungeonReady.lua:296) 0, h/2-260 fixed No No
Key holder text keyHolder (DungeonReady.lua:305) 0, h/2-90 fixed No No
Loadout reminder popup loadoutReminder (LoadoutReminder.lua:178) 0, h/2-180 fixed No No
Token alert tokenAlert (TokenAlert.lua:53) 0, h/2-160 fixed No No
Mythic+ timer (Tracker, shown on QoL) keystone (Tracker/Keystone.lua:507-521) to minimap TOPRIGHT→BOTTOMRIGHT y-8 tracker.keyWidth/keyHeight/keyScale No Yes. Width, height and scale rows are on QoL > Mythic+ and in the mover popup.
Cursor ring/trail none (follows the cursor) - qol.cursorSize n/a Yes
Blizzard windows (Move.lua) not Layout. Separate store qol.positions[frameName] (Move.lua:40-42) and qol.scales[frameName] (Move.lua:107), written by dragging and Ctrl+wheel, cleared by "Reset windows" (Move.lua:174-175) Blizzard default qol.scales No rows (switches only) No rows

Summary of how options and the mover relate:

  • No Minimap or QoL options row writes x/y. Position always lives in profile.layouts.Default.<key>, except Blizzard windows (qol.positions) and in-map placement enums (text places, button spots/angle/push, drawer side), which are a second placement system.
  • Size lives only in module keys (minimap.size/scale/borderSize, difficultyScale, reminderSize, tracker.key*). The mover never stores size; it re-applies its anchor when the frame resizes (Layout.lua:987-993).
  • The mover popup exposes a hand-picked subset of the page rows by label (PickSpecs). Because the match is by label, renamed labels break it silently (P13), and generic labels are fragile (P18).
  • Widgets/PlaceEditor.lua and Widgets/Positions.lua are not used by either module. Only GroupFrames uses them (GroupFrames/Preview.lua:659, GroupFrames/Options.lua:115,282,432, GroupFrames/Buttons.lua:400,448). Minimap keeps its own place enums (M.TEXT_PLACES, M.BUTTON_SPOTS, M.BUTTON_SPOTS_ANGLE, M.DRAWER_SIDES).

Part 6

LixardUI audit: settings window core + move mode

Repo LixardUI, build 0.0.854 (LixardUI_Options/LixardUI_Options.toc:5). Read-only audit; nothing was changed. Paths are relative to the repo root. Options.lua means LixardUI_Options/Options.lua.

PART A: settings window core

A1. How the window is put together

A1.1 Page registration (three ways in)

  1. Core pages in LixardUI_Options/Pages/*.lua call O:AddPage(page) (Options.lua:159-164). The toc load order is Builder, Options, Share, Wizard, General, Modules, Skins, Extra, Profiles, About (LixardUI_Options.toc:12-21). The whole options addon is LoadOnDemand (toc:9) and loads through WithOptions (LixardUI/Core/Entry.lua:40-49).
  2. Module pages: each module calls ns:AddOptionsPage(page) (Entry.lua:25-32) when its own addon loads. That call drops the page if retailOnly is set on a non-retail client, filters retailOnly/foreverOnly sections and specs (Entry.lua:15-23), and stores the page in ns.extraPages. Pages/Extra.lua then works through the list: - page.into merges a page into another one, renaming its subs with subNames (Extra.lua:7-17). Only GroupFrames does this, into unitframes (LixardUI_GroupFrames/Options.lua:637). - spec.skipIf() removes specs (Extra.lua:19-29). - section.home/homeSub/homeTab/homeTitle move a section to another page (Extra.lua:31-48). - page.subOrder sorts the subs (Extra.lua:50-62). - O:AddPage registers the pages that were not merged, and merged keys get an alias (Extra.lua:64-70).
  3. Look specs: ns:AddLookSpecs(title, specs, sub) (Entry.lua:11-13) adds module look controls such as corners, borders, bar textures, fonts and backgrounds. Pages/General.lua:313-324 hands them out: - The HOMES table (General.lua:271-293) sends most entries back to the module's own page (MoveHome, General.lua:295-311). - Only entries with no home stay on General, under the subs Look, Borders and corners, Fonts, Bars, Colors or Backgrounds (General.lua:263). - Today the one entry with no home is "Mythic+ timer" bar texture (LixardUI_Tracker/Options.lua:622), so it lands in General > Bars. - The CHAPTERS list names "Colors" and "Backgrounds" views for General (Options.lua:43-44). A view is only created when its sub exists (Options.lua:241), so these two views normally do not appear.

The page model is { key, title, description, sections = { { title, sub, tab, columns, preview, specs = { spec... } } } }. A spec is { type, label|labelText, desc|descText, get, set, options, min/max/step/format, disabled, disabledNote, advanced, tip, confirm, kind, width, onClick, onSubmit, onPick, build, skipIf, retailOnly, foreverOnly }. Builder reads these in Builder.lua:57-146 and Builder.lua:154-176.

Specs carry no SV key path and no default value. Every spec has only get/set closures. This is the main gap for "differs from default" markers and per-tab reset (see A1.6).

A1.2 Chapters, views, subs, tabs, folds

  • Chapters are 8 hard-coded top tabs: Look, Frames, Buttons & buffs, Combat & groups, World, Social, Items & windows, System (Options.lua:37-88).
  • Each lists views as { pageKey, subName, label=, each= }.
  • BuildViews (Options.lua:207-267) turns these into view buttons:
    • a named sub gives only = { sub };
    • each = true gives one view per sub that is not claimed elsewhere;
    • no sub gives the whole page minus subs claimed elsewhere (skip).
  • Pages that no chapter claims fall into System (Options.lua:257-263).
  • The last view per chapter is remembered in global.navLast[chapterTitle] (Options.lua:93-101, 733, 981).
  • Subs are built by Index(page) (Options.lua:122-157). It groups sections by section.sub. If no section has a sub and there are 2 or more sections, each section title becomes its own sub. InView shows only the sections of the current sub or view (Options.lua:178-187).
  • Tabs are dead in the UI.
  • Index builds sub.tabs from section.tab (Options.lua:140-153), and 117 specs/sections use tab = L[...].
  • However, ShowTabs is only ever called with nil (Options.lua:737, 842), so the tab bar never appears (Options.lua:1182-1211).
  • InView never checks tabKey, so the sections of every tab render together in the sub. The tab name is only added to the fold title (GroupLabel, Options.lua:438) and used to pick a preview (Options.lua:304).
  • Result: a "tab" today is just a fold header prefix. Per-tab reset would in practice be per-fold-group or per-sub.
  • Folds (groups): every section is a card with a clickable head (chevron, upper-case title, count, seam line) (Options.lua:514-588).
  • Open state is stored in global.optionsGroups["page|sub|groupLabel"] (Options.lua:422-431, 507-512). The first section of each sub is open by default (section.firstGroup, Options.lua:611).
  • A section whose label equals the page heading is drawn "bare", with no head (Options.lua:468-487).
  • The tray has "Open all folds" and "Fold all" links (Options.lua:1457-1460, 1090-1100) and an "On this page" index of the group heads (Options.lua:1102-1131).
  • Probable bug: fold state likely does not survive a reload.
    • ns.defaults.global.optionsGroups = {} is assigned at Options load (Options.lua:90), after DB:Init already attached global (LixardUI/Core/Database.lua:111).
    • Until a raw table exists, ns.DB.global.optionsGroups resolves through __index to the defaults table itself. Writes in Options.lua:510/720/726/1053/1096 therefore go into ns.defaults, and nothing is saved.
    • navLast avoids this on purpose with rawget + create (Options.lua:93-101). optionsGroups should do the same, or be registered with DB:AddDefaults.
    • Not verified in game.
  • Row layout:
  • Short controls (switch, check, color, button) pair into 2 columns when there is room. section.columns = 2 forces 2 columns and columns = 1 forces 1 (Options.lua:366-420).
  • Runs of sliders become a 2–3 column grid with the label stacked over the slider (Options.lua:392-397, Builder.lua:188-203).
  • Preview tray: section.preview is a builder fn(canvas) -> { frame, Refresh } (Options.lua:299-364). It has a Bigger/Smaller toggle stored in global.previewLarge (Options.lua:35, 1562-1566). cooldowns is always large (Options.lua:31).
  • Footer (Options.lua:1300-1358): profile dropdown, Import/Export links, a "Show help text" switch, "Preview group" (/lui grouptest) and "Move frames" (closes the window and calls ns.Layout:ToggleStandalone()).
  • Window: 1200x760, fit to the screen (W.FitScreen, Options.lua:1469-1471), draggable from the top bar, chapter bar, view bar, header and footer. Its position is saved in global.optionsPosition (Options.lua:936-953). Esc closes it through UISpecialFrames (Options.lua:1478).
  • Reload bar appears when ns:NeedsReload() (Options.lua:906-912, 1538-1548; LixardUI/Core/Modules.lua:119-127).
  • Deep links: O:OpenAt(key, sub, tab) (Options.lua:1624-1636), O.Jump(pageKey, sectionTitle, label) (Options.lua:960-975), and ns.OpenOptionsAt (Entry.lua:61-63), which the move dialog's "All settings" button uses.

A1.3 "Advanced"

  • spec.advanced = true is read in BuildPage (Options.lua:595-640).
  • When the page uses subs, or fewer than 2 sections hold advanced specs (Options.lua:608), advanced specs are pulled out into one extra fold per sub, titled "Advanced" (Options.lua:615-625). That fold starts closed because firstGroup is unset (Options.lua:426-431).
  • Otherwise (no subs and 2 or more sections with advanced specs) they stay inline (keepAdvanced, Options.lua:613, 581, 630).
  • There is no "show advanced" switch. ns.defaults.global.showAdvanced = false (Options.lua:33) and ShowAdvanced() (Options.lua:189-191) exist, but nothing calls ShowAdvanced(). Advanced settings are always reachable, only folded. Dead code.
  • Visible() always returns true (Options.lua:193-195). It is a dead filter used by search (Options.lua:792).

A1.4 "Show help"

  • global.showHelp (default false, Options.lua:34) is toggled by the footer switch (Options.lua:1329-1338).
  • On: each row shows its desc under the label (Builder.lua:185-186, 217-225), and the page header shows page.description (Options.lua:700).
  • Off: hovering a row shows desc in a GameTooltip instead (Builder.lua:257-268), and so does a control's own spec.tip (Builder.lua:302-304).
  • Wizard rows use the same Builder, so turning help off also hides the wizard descriptions (Wizard.lua:185, Builder.lua:185).
  • Move-dialog rows are compact and never show desc inline, only as a tooltip (Builder.lua:159, 185, 288-291).
  • disabledNote is added to the label in a hard-coded |cff6e6555 color (Builder.lua:171-175) and in the tooltip as literal 1, 0.82, 0.3 (Builder.lua:265).

A1.5 Search today

  • Box: W.SearchBox in the top bar (Options.lua:1261-1274), 310 px wide (Options.lua:29). It searches on every keystroke (OnTextChanged, Options.lua:1265-1268). Enter opens the first result (Options.lua:1269-1274).
  • No keyboard shortcut: no "/" or Ctrl+F focus, and no OnKeyDown/EnableKeyboard anywhere in LixardUI_Options, Layout or Widgets (grep: no matches). The box has to be clicked. Esc only clears focus, not the text (LixardUI/Widgets/EditBox.lua:78).
  • Overlap: Options.lua:1263-1264 places a magnifier icon at RIGHT -9, but W.SearchBox puts its clear (X) button at RIGHT -3 (EditBox.lua:126-137). With text typed, the two icons overlap.
  • Fields matched (Score, Options.lua:791-803):
  • label (labelText resolved, or label);
  • desc (descText resolved, or desc);
  • "place" = page title, section title, subKey, tabKey, groupLabel;
  • every dropdown option text (OptionTexts, Options.lua:756-770; function options are called with pcall).
  • Not matched: spec.tip, button text, disabledNote, current values, chapter and view titles. A view label like "Window material" is therefore not searchable. groupLabel is only set once a page is built (Options.lua:517), so for pages not opened yet the place text lacks it.
  • Matching rules:
  • Every word must match (AND).
  • Naive suffix stemming: ation, ions, ion, ing, ers, er, ed, es, s, e (Options.lua:778-789).
  • Score per word: label +3, place +2, other +1.
  • Results are sorted by score, then by registration order.
  • type = "text" specs are skipped (Options.lua:850).
  • Advanced specs are included, because the search walks the raw page.sections.
  • Results UI (Options.lua:811-888):
  • Up to 150 rows (MAX_RESULTS, Options.lua:14). Each row is 52 px with title, path "Page › Sub › Tab › Section" and desc.
  • The page header turns into Search / "query" / N settings match.
  • Clicking a row clears the search and calls ShowPage(page, spec, section). That opens the fold holding the row, scrolls to it and flashes it for 2.5 s (Options.lua:716-727, 743-753; Builder.lua:247-255).
  • The result path uses page.title, not the chapter or view names the user sees in the navigation.

A1.6 Reset features that exist

Feature Where What it resets
Reset this profile Pages/Profiles.lua:97-107 -> DB:ResetProfile (Database.lua:185-190) Wipes the whole profile and reattaches defaults
Copy from another profile Profiles.lua:87-95 -> DB:CopyProfile (Database.lua:170-183) Replaces the whole profile
Reset highlight color Pages/General.lua:63-73 profile.accent.r/g/b = nil (Theme.lua:318-323)
Border color everywhere General.lua:241-251 -> Theme:SetBorderEverywhere (Theme.lua:371-384) moduleBorders[*].mode = "default", tooltips.border = "default"
Corners (everywhere) General.lua:225-238 -> Theme:SetCornersEverywhere (Theme.lua:350-369) Overwrites 9 module copies plus the minimap shape
Reset this text LixardUI/Widgets/TextStyle.lua:222 -> TS:Reset (TextStyle.lua:138-141) profile.textStyles[id] = nil
Module resets e.g. LixardUI_UnitFrames/Options.lua:79, LixardUI_GroupFrames/Options.lua:126/296/446, LixardUI_Bags/Options.lua:171, LixardUI_Cooldowns/Options.lua:96, LixardUI_QoL/Options.lua:60, LixardUI_ActionBars/Options.lua:96 Positions, order, sections or windows; see Part B

There is no per-setting, per-section, per-sub or per-tab reset. The only confirm pattern is "click twice within 4 s" (Builder.lua:24-41, Toolbar.lua:194-209, Wizard.lua:292-307).

A1.7 How defaults merge (LixardUI/Core/Database.lua)

  • Scopes: profile (per named profile, in LixardUIDB.profiles[name]), global (LixardUIDB.global) and char (LixardUICharDB) (Database.lua:89-114). Core defaults are profile = { nameParts = "full" } and char = { installed = false } (LixardUI/Core/Defaults.lua:3-9).
  • Adding defaults:
  • Core files assign into ns.defaults.<scope> at file load, before DB:Init (e.g. Theme.lua:16-23, TextStyle.lua:7-8, BarTexture.lua:8, Layout.lua:15-16).
  • Modules call ns.DB:AddDefaults(scope, key, value) (Database.lua:116-124), which also re-attaches when the value is a table.
  • Options-addon files assign directly (Options.lua:33-35, 90; General.lua:8). That is fine for scalars but not for optionsGroups (see A1.2).
  • Attach (Database.lua:20-30): for every table default it makes sure a raw subtable exists (rawset), recurses into it, and sets the metatable { __index = defaults } (cached per defaults table, Database.lua:9-18).
  • Scalars are never copied; they are read through __index.
  • Raw presence means "written this session or kept from the last save", not "differs from default".
  • Strip (Database.lua:32-45): for each key in defaults:
  • nested tables are stripped recursively, and an empty table is removed;
  • a scalar equal to the default is removed.
  • Keys not in defaults (e.g. moduleBorders[module], textStyles[id], layouts, blizzardMovers[key] entries, skins.enabled[key]) are always kept, even when they hold a "default-equivalent" value.
  • When Strip runs: only on DB:Shutdown at PLAYER_LOGOUT (Database.lua:204-212, LixardUI/Core/Start.lua:55) and on profile switch (Database.lua:131-133). During a session, a value set back to its default stays raw in the table. Fallback (Database.lua:47-55) only restores metatables after the logout strip.
  • Answer to "is only non-default saved?": Yes, on disk, for keys that have a default. No, in memory: a raw value can equal the default. A "differs from default" marker must therefore compare the value with the defaults table, not test rawget.

  • Caveats for a marker or reset feature: 1. Never set a table key to nil to "reset" it. After t.sub = nil, t.sub resolves to the shared defaults table, and the next t.sub.x = v writes into ns.defaults. This is the same trap as optionsGroups. Reset a table by wipe(raw) and then re-Attach. DB:ResetProfile does this correctly (Database.lua:185-190). 2. Color arrays get stripped element-wise. For example, frameBorder.color = {0.25,0.65,1} (Theme.lua:18) saved as {0.25,0.5,1} is stored as {[2]=0.5}. Reads through __index still work, but #, ipairs and unpack on the raw table do not. A per-setting compare must compare per component. 3. Many specs have no 1:1 key, for example:

    • BorderSpecs maps accent to custom (BorderSpecs.lua:29-33);
    • TextStyle "global" means nil (TextStyle.lua:143-182);
    • skins.enabled[key] nil means on (LixardUI_Skins/Engine.lua:21-23);
    • Modules switches live in C_AddOns enable state, not in SV (LixardUI/Core/Modules.lua:56-58, 100-108);
    • the accent is 3 keys plus class.

    So a generic "differs" marker needs an explicit key/path (plus default/isDefault/reset) field on each spec. It cannot be derived from get/set. - Profile export (LixardUI/Core/Share.lua:45-62) walks the raw saved profile with pairs. It exports explicitly set values only (including set-to-default values not stripped yet), and no global data such as layoutTools, scale, showHelp or fold state.

A2. Inventory: core pages and wizard

Legend: adv = advanced = true. "affects" names the code that reads the value.

Manage Copy from another profile overwrites the current profile pick (dropdown + Copy, confirm) – – DB:CopyProfile
Manage Reset this profile wipes the current profile button (danger, confirm) – – DB:ResetProfile
Manage Delete a profile profiles[name] = nil pick (danger, confirm) – – DB:DeleteProfile

The footer also has a profile dropdown and Import/Export (Options.lua:1316-1324). That duplicates "Active profile".

About (Pages/About.lua, key about)

Section Label Storage Widget

LixardUI Memory and CPU use – button -> ns.Perf:Report()

/lui move, /lui setup, /lui export and similar are not listed (compare the full list in Core/Commands.lua:20-61).

Wizard (LixardUI_Options/Wizard.lua)

640x500, centered, draggable. The position is not saved (Wizard.lua:250-261). It does not use W.FitScreen and is not in UISpecialFrames. Combat makes it wait (Wizard.lua:328-333). It runs automatically at login when char.installed is false (Entry.lua:117-123).

Step Label Storage Widget Notes
1 Welcome – – text
2 Profile Profile for this character profileKeys[char] dropdown
2 Profile Or start a new profile new profiles[name] input Silently does nothing if the name exists (Wizard.lua:101-106). Profiles page prints a message instead (Profiles.lua:51-54)
3 Look Quick picks profile.accent.r/g/b custom: 7 hard-coded dots (Wizard.lua:12-20, 34-59) The first dot is blue {0.25,0.65,1}. The house default amber is not among the picks. The hover ring uses literal 1,1,1 (Wizard.lua:44, 50-51)
3 Look Use my class color profile.accent.class switch Same text as General
3 Look Any color profile.accent.r/g/b color Duplicate of General > Highlight color

There is no wizard step for font, corners, border, bar texture, UI scale or "move frames". The separator line at Wizard.lua:286-290 is a texture with no SetTexture/SetColorTexture. It is tinted only, so it may be invisible (unsure).

A4. Widget set (LixardUI/Widgets/*.lua)

File Constructor(s) Look / behaviour
Panel.lua W.Panel/Decorate (:172-195), W.Window (:224-237), W.Glyph (:251), W.Overlay (:264), W.Icon/IconPath (:283-296), W.Slit (:215), W.Pill (:470), W.Tooltip/ShowTooltip (:320-338), W.WindowTitle/WindowClose (:342-356), W.FitScreen (:383), W.PreviewStage (:391), W.Sound, W.Dim Base of everything: a 9-slice rounded fill plus an edge ring from Media/Textures/hide_ch<N>. Colors are theme keys. Windows get a woven/scales pattern and a stitched seam (:53-127). W.SmokedGlass/W.NoGlass are empty stubs that only hide luiGlass (:197-203)

Slider.lua W.Slider(parent, text, min, max, step, get, set, width, fmt) (:176-234) 2 px seam track, textDim fill, 12 px brass diamond thumb, 56 px number box (type a value, Enter/Esc). Drag runs an OnUpdate only while dragging. Mouse wheel always changes the value (:128-132, :222), so scrolling the settings page over a slider changes settings. Inconsistent with the scroll frame
Dropdown.lua W.Dropdown(parent, text, options, get, set, width) (:217-250) 24 px raised field plus arrow. One shared menu (max 12 rows, wheel scroll, "n-m / N" counter, check icon on the current option), click-catcher, flips up near the screen bottom. No search inside long lists such as LSM fonts or textures

Not used by the settings window, whose tabs are underline style (Options.lua:1157-1180) and never shown anyway
Button.lua W.Button(parent, text, width, onClick, kind, height) (:120-141), W.CloseButton (:114), W.StyleClose (:91), W.PlaceClose (:107) Kinds: normal, primary (bone fill, ink text), danger, quiet, ghost, close (:5-12). Auto-fits to the label (Fit)
EditBox.lua W.EditBox (:68), W.SearchBox (:122), W.SelectAllOnFocus (:62) "edge" fill with a seam border, placeholder text with a truncation tooltip. SearchBox adds a clear X, shown by an OnUpdate poll (:139) that runs every frame while visible
Scroll.lua W.Scroll (:86), W.SkinScrollBar (:128) Scroll area with content, SetContentHeight, ScrollTo
BorderSpecs.lua W.BorderSpecs(module, label, desc) (:22-58), W.PickSpecs(page, labels) (:60-74) Spec factory (dropdown plus color) for global or per-module border; PickSpecs reuses page specs by label (used by the move dialog)
TextStyle.lua ns.TextStyles with Define/Attach/Resolve/Apply/Specs/GlobalSpecs/Reset/Check Spec factory: per-text Font, Size, Outline, Shadow, Color, Custom color, Show, Reset (:143-224)
BarTexture.lua W.BarTextureChoices, W.BarTextureFile, W.UseBarTexture, W.RefreshBarTextures, W.BarTextureSpec(store, field, after, extra) (:21-97) Registry plus a dropdown spec factory; "default" = follow General
Positions.lua W.POSITIONS, W.POSITION_OPTIONS, W.INSIDE_*, W.PlaceRegion, W.SpotXY, W.InsidePlace, W.PlaceOptions (:6-108) 23 named anchor spots for sub-elements (texts and icons on a frame), not screen movers
PlaceEditor.lua W.PlaceEditor(stage, opts) (:54-252) Drag-to-place editor inside a preview: hover ring, dot targets, snap radius 14, right-click reset. Writes through opts.set/reset/commit
Others IconPicker, IconSearch, Money (W.MoneyInput, W.Confirm), Prompt, VirtualList, CastBar, HealthExtras (spec factories W.HealthExtrasSpecs, W.SharedHealthSpecs, W.ExtraColorSpecs), RoleIcon (W.RoleSpecs, W.SharedRoleSpecs), AuraHide (W.AuraHideSpecs)
  • Builder control types (LixardUI_Options/Builder.lua:57-146): switch, check, slider, dropdown, color, button, input (EditBox plus button), pick (dropdown plus button), text, custom (any frame through spec.build).
  • Segmented control: none as a Builder type. W.Tabs looks like one but is window navigation only. Choice settings are always dropdowns.
  • Keybind widget: none in Widgets (grep KeyBind|keybind in Widgets and Options: none). Key binding is a separate hover-to-bind mode in ActionBars (ns.KeyBindMode, Core/Commands.lua:192-198, Toolbar.lua:304-311).

Inconsistencies 1. Tabs come in two styles: segment (Tabs.lua) and underline (Options.lua:1157-1180, never shown). 2. Close buttons are made three ways: - W.CloseButton(bar, fn, 24), where the extra 24 argument is ignored (Options.lua:1257, Button.lua:114); - W.Button(...,"",22,...) + W.Icon + W.StyleClose (Dialog.lua:69-74, LixardUI_Options/Share.lua:57-62); - the Wizard has no close button at all. 3. Disabled look: controls use SetAlpha(0.6) (5 widgets, e.g. Toggle.lua:77, Slider.lua:52, Dropdown.lua:41, ColorSwatch.lua:27), while Tokens.alpha.disabled = 0.45 (Tokens.lua:44) is never used. 4. Window shells differ: - Options: W.Panel(..."window","edge","large") (Options.lua:1469); - Wizard, Share and Dialog: border "frame" (Wizard.lua:251, Share.lua:37, Dialog.lua:58).

So the user's border color reaches the wizard and dialogs but not the main settings window. Only Wizard and Share get W.Window (strip). The Dialog uses radius "medium". 5. Titles: Options uses a 34 px heading font (Options.lua:1506); Wizard 20 px body font (Wizard.lua:163); Share 15 px accent (Share.lua:50); Dialog 14 px accent (Dialog.lua:65); W.WindowTitle 16 px heading (Panel.lua:340-349). 6. The slider mouse wheel is always active (above). The dropdown menu has no keyboard navigation. 7. The help tooltip on rows uses GameTooltip with literal 1,1,1 and 1,0.82,0.3 (Builder.lua:262, 265), not theme keys.

A5. Tokens (LixardUI/Theme/Tokens.lua) and hard-coded values

What Tokens holds (Tokens.lua:16-118): - colors: 22 hex colors (hide*, seam, line, edge, bone, dust, ash, eye, scale, brass, good, warn, bad, info, gold, ink); - alpha: window .97, highlight .08, disabled .45, dimRow .6, filtered .25, woven .07, scales .10, shadow .9; - fonts: heading/body/bodyBold/numbers files; size 13, titleSize 14, xs 10 / sm 11 / md 13 / lg 15 / xl 18 / page 34; - space: hair 2, tight 4, gap 8, pad 14, section 24; - size: control 24, row 26, rowTight 22, title 34, tab 25, close 22, closeInset 8, search 26, slot 36, scute 6, slit 6x13, shadow 3; - corner: 2/4/7; - border: edge/seam/seamInset/quality/dispel; - textures: 15 media files plus WHITE8X8; - crop: {.08,.92}.

The theme copies colors, radius and fonts out of Tokens (Theme.lua:404-435).

Tokens that are never read (grep across the repo, Dev excluded): - all of fonts.xs/sm/md/lg/xl/page; - all of space.*; - size.control/row/rowTight/title/tab/search/slot/shadow; - alpha.disabled/dimRow/filtered/highlight/shadow; - crop.

Only size.close, size.closeInset, size.scute, size.slit* (Button.lua, Panel.lua) and alpha.woven/scales (Panel.lua:106-111) are used. Tokens.colors is read directly (outside the theme) only in Friends (Raid.lua:12-13, Window.lua:612-614) and Vendor (Rows.lua:15-16).

Hard-coded sizes and layout: - 51 W.Text(..., <literal size>) calls in Options/Widgets/Layout (grep W.Text([^)]*, *[0-9]), e.g. Options.lua:817 (13), 819 (11), 1243 (18), 1506 (34). - Builder constants: CONTROL_WIDTH=230, ROW_HEIGHT=38, CONTROL_HEIGHT=24, GAP=10 (Builder.lua:10-14). - The Options layout block (Options.lua:11-30) duplicates Tokens.size.control/search and others.

Literal colors (SetColorTexture/SetVertexColor/SetTextColor with numbers): - Options.lua:1573, 1581 (preview canvas glow, painted then cleared to 0 alpha; a leftover); - Wizard.lua:44, 50-51; - Layout.lua:697 (hovered mover edge white .9); - Toolbar.lua:73-77 (grid lines), 116 (fill alpha .96), 226 (divider, later tinted); - Widgets/CastBar.lua:80, 312; - HealthExtras.lua:307, 343-353, 392-399, 413, 420, 1005; - RoleIcon.lua:81; - TextStyle.lua:96 (shadow black); - Panel.lua:482.

Literal |cff codes: - Builder.lua:174 (6e6555 = ash); - Pages/Modules.lua:22 (ffc840, 8a8f99, not token colors); - Layout.lua:712 (a49884 = dust); - Money.lua:44-49; - Core/Commands.lua:6-16, 76, 411 (55ff55, ff5555, ffd200, a49884); - Core/Init.lua:13 (brand 3fa7ff blue, which also appears in the toc title LixardUI_Options.toc:2. Neither is a house color).

Literal paths: - Theme.lua:25 hard-codes "Interface\\AddOns\\LixardUI\\Media\\Fonts\\". Tokens.lua:3-4 derives the same path from addonName, and BUILT_IN_FONTS (Theme.lua:26-37) repeats the Tokens font files. - WHITE8X8 is re-declared in BarTexture.lua:6, CastBar.lua:6, HealthExtras.lua:5, Panel.lua:482 and Toolbar.lua:19, while Tokens.textures.white exists. - Blizzard bar paths are repeated in BarTexture.lua:12-14 and HealthExtras.lua:6-11, 294.

Mover color: the mover box and edge use the class color (Layout.lua:680-685), ignoring both the accent and the theme.

A3b. Per-module use of the shared look settings

Source: a sweep of every module's defaults and its font, texture and border use. Spot-checked; module-relative paths are under LixardUI_<Module>/.

Headlines - Body font: every module follows profile.fonts.body through Theme:Font("normal"). No module hard-codes a font file; the only literal Fonts\\ paths are commented lines in the bundled LibActionButton. - profile.frameText (outline, shadow, size) is effectively a UnitFrames + GroupFrames only setting. - Only those two attach texts through ns.TextStyles:Attach. UF ids are uf.<key>.<part>; GF ids come from G.TextStyleID(kind) (exact string not resolved). - Every other module passes a literal "OUTLINE" to W.Text/SetFont. Chat, Tracker and Minimap have their own outline keys. - frameText.scale only reaches attached texts (TextStyle.lua:77). So the General rows "Frame text font/outline/shadow/size" promise more than they deliver. - Corners: 9 modules keep a copy that SetCornersEverywhere overwrites (Theme.lua:338-342). Auction, Chat, Friends, Mail, Maps, Meters, QoL, Skins and Vendor read the global profile.corners directly. ActionBars adds its own radius (10) and Minimap its own corner ("medium"). - Border: - Most modules expose W.BorderSpecs("<module>"). - Meters (frame:meters) and Skins (frame:skins) read a module key but offer no selector. - Maps and QoL have no module border at all. - Tooltips uses its own tooltips.border dropdown with "default". - GroupFrames shares the unitframes key. - Minimap's map edge has its own borderColor/customColor/borderSize, separate from moduleBorders.minimap, which only covers the container. - Bar texture: - These follow the global: UF unitframes.barTexture="default", centerResources.texture (falls back to the UF key), altPower.barTexture, castBarTexture, DataBars, Meters, Tooltips, Tracker keystone, Cooldowns manager.*.barTexture. - GroupFrames' own barTexture is likely shadowed by UF's key whenever profile.unitframes exists (GroupFrames/Buttons.lua:150-158, unsure). - Bypasses: Cooldowns flatBars=true uses literal WHITE8X8 (Cooldowns/Cooldowns.lua:94-95), QoL Summon.lua:60, Skins Professions.lua:65.

Module Font follows global Outline follows frameText Bar texture global Border follows General (key) Corners Own scale/size keys

Look specs that a module exports through ns:AddLookSpecs appear on the module's own page through HOMES (General.lua:271-293): ActionBars Options.lua:317, Auction :50, Auras :103, Bags :583, Chat :233/235/236, Cooldowns :222-223, DataBars :305-306, Friends :176, Mail :77, Meters :191-192, Minimap :496/498, Tooltips :143-146, Tracker :618-622, UnitFrames :449-458/524/759-769, Vendor :50.

PART B: move / unlock mode

B1. How it works

Entry points - /lui move -> ns.Layout:ToggleStandalone() (LixardUI/Core/Commands.lua:261). - Settings footer "Move frames" (Options.lua:1340-1343). - Blizzard Edit Mode: EditMode.Enter/Exit callbacks (LixardUI/Layout/Layout.lua:1198-1206, 1158-1167).

Standalone vs Edit Mode - ToggleStandalone refuses while Edit Mode has it active (Layout.lua:1133-1142). - On entering it snapshots the current layout (snapshot = Copy(Saved(false)), Layout.lua:1138). - SetActive(on) shows every mover (ShowMover respects options.shown() and onlyInLayoutMode, Layout.lua:1028-1039) and sends LIXARDUI_LAYOUT_MODE (Layout.lua:1098-1125). - The toolbar, grid and Cancel are standalone only (Toolbar.lua:341-355). In Edit Mode only the mover boxes appear. Grid snap then uses Blizzard's grid when Blizzard snap is on (Layout.lua:453-463).

Mover box (CreateMover, Layout.lua:915-974) - A Button covering the target (or options.cover(mover)), strata DIALOG. - Shows the name label, and coordinates on hover (ShowCoords, Layout.lua:183-204). - Gear and pin ("attach") buttons appear on hover. - Colors come from the class color (Layout.lua:680-703). See-through mode lowers the fill alpha.

Dragging - Mouse down starts an OnUpdate drag with a 3 px threshold (Layout.lua:652-674, 781-808). Positions are clamped to the screen (Layout.lua:546-556). - Mouse up calls Store, which rounds to whole pixels and saves (Layout.lua:297-322). - Arrow keys nudge the selected mover by 1 px, Shift+arrow by 10 px. This uses override bindings on a hidden button; they are cleared on deselect and after combat (Layout.lua:810-842).

Storage - profile.layouts[LayoutName()][key] (Layout.lua:15, 40-49, 67-70). - LayoutName() is "Default", or the legacy name with the most entries (Layout.lua:24-38). It is not tied to the Blizzard Edit Mode layout. - Plain spot: { point, x, y }, relative to the nearest of 9 screen anchors (ToAnchor, Layout.lua:82-96). - Attached spot: { anchor=<otherKey>, side, x, y, fallback={point,x,y} } (Link, Layout.lua:264-277). - Module default specs can also pin: { to, point, relPoint, x, y, axis, fallback } (Pinned, Layout.lua:111-132). - Dependents follow their parent (ApplyDependents, Layout.lua:218-229). Cycles are prevented (DependsOn/CanAttach, Layout.lua:147-156, 330-332). - Layout stores no size or scale. Size and scale live in module keys (see B2).

Snapping (Snap, Layout.lua:579-634) - Shift = free movement. - Frame/screen snap distance is 12 px standalone and 8 px in Edit Mode (Layout.lua:11-12, 581). - Targets are the screen edges and center plus every other visible mover's edges and center. Movers that touch are weighted 0.5 (Layout.lua:465-519). Movers in the same snapGroup (action bars) use real halves (Layout.lua:587-592). - Grid snap applies within 8 px, or always while Ctrl is held (Layout.lua:616-631). - The grid spacing is layoutTools.gridSize in screen pixels (Layout.lua:445-451). Guide lines in the "accent" color show the line being snapped to (Layout.lua:395-432). - Toolbar switches turn snapGrid and snapFrames on or off (Toolbar.lua:273-279).

Toolbar (LixardUI/Layout/Toolbar.lua:232-333) - A slanted drop-down bar at the top center: - Grid on/off; - Grid size 8–64 step 4; - Grid lightness 0–100; - Grid color (white/class/accent); - Snap to grid; - Snap to frames; - Coordinates; - See-through; - Key binding (leaves move mode and starts ns.KeyBindMode); - Reset all (click twice); - Cancel; - Save; - Auto-hide (tucks the bar into a "Move frames" tab). - All of these are stored in global.layoutTools (Layout.lua:16). This is account-wide while positions are per profile. - A readout under the bar shows the screen description, or "name x y snap-state" while dragging (Toolbar.lua:357-368).

Save / Cancel / Reset - Save just leaves move mode (Toolbar.lua:299); every move was already written live in Store. - Cancel (Layout:CancelChanges, Layout.lua:1144-1149) restores layouts[name] from the snapshot and re-applies. It does not undo: - gear-dialog settings (sizes, scales, Hide it, module rows); - toolbar settings; - Stack/Match buttons used from the settings window. - Edit Mode sessions have no snapshot and no Cancel. Blizzard's "revert" does not cover LixardUI moves. - Reset all (Layout.lua:1151-1156) deletes positions only; sizes stay. - Gear Reset position (Dialog.lua:84-88 -> Layout:Reset, Layout.lua:381-391). - There is no Esc handling for move mode: nothing registers in UISpecialFrames or a key handler.

Combat - Move mode cannot open in combat (Layout.lua:1101-1104). - Entering combat closes it and reopens it after combat (Layout.lua:1169-1184). - Place queues protected frames through ns.Combat:Run (Layout.lua:206-211). - Nudging is ignored in combat (Layout.lua:816). - Screen-size changes re-apply through ns.Combat:Run (Layout.lua:1234-1242). - Blizzard movers queue in combat (Blizzard.lua file lines 50-53).

Edit Mode interplay - Entering Edit Mode turns on the LixardUI movers. Leaving turns them off and re-applies on the next frame (Layout.lua:1158-1167). - Selecting a Blizzard system deselects the LixardUI mover (hook on EditModeManagerFrame.SelectSystem, Layout.lua:1204-1206). - EDIT_MODE_LAYOUTS_UPDATED re-applies all (Layout.lua:1186-1189, 1249). - Positions do not change when the user switches Edit Mode layouts.

Diagnostics left running - global.layoutLog: an 80-entry ring buffer of debugstack strings, written on every store/attach/reset/cancel (Layout.lua:279-295). - global.layoutProbe: written at every login, plus snapshots of every mover at 2 s, 8 s and logout (Layout.lua:1209, 1212-1232). - Both persist in SavedVariables.

Unregister (Layout.lua:1014-1026) deletes the saved spot. Only DataBars uses it, when a text bar is deleted (LixardUI_DataBars/Panels.lua:674), which is intended.

B2. The gear dialog (LixardUI/Layout/Dialog.lua)

Fixed content (Dialog.lua:57-100) - Title; - Left/right and Up/down number fields (pixels from the screen center; Enter commits through Layout:Move); - Reset position; - "All settings": leaves standalone and calls ns.OpenOptionsAt(page, sub or mover.name, tab) (Dialog.lua:42-55); hidden when there is no options.page; - a hint line.

Module content - options.settings() returns spec rows, built with O.Builder.Row(..., { compact = true, controlWidth = 170 }) inside a scroll area capped at 85 % of the screen height (Dialog.lua:102-120, 146-179). - Opening any gear therefore loads LixardUI_Options (Dialog.lua:106). - Each panel is built once per mover per session and cached, including false (Dialog.lua:103-109). - The dialog sits next to the mover, on the left or right (Dialog.lua:181-192).

Specs are given in one of two ways - W.PickSpecs(page, labels) returns the same spec objects as the options page (BorderSpecs.lua:60-74). It takes the first label match and drops missing labels silently. Used by Tooltips, Minimap frame and buttons, QoL BuffReminder/BattleRes/GroupTools, Tracker and Keystone. - Otherwise the module rebuilds a copy of the page specs through its builder function. The copy writes the same SV key.

Every registered mover

SV paths are under profile.. The position is always at layouts.Default.<key>.

Look and behaviour options inside gear dialogs (not position or size): - Look: Background opacity and Tab opacity (chat); Bar texture (meters, encounter bar); Shape (minimap); Faded opacity and Opacity (bars, resources); Text size (data text, group frames, meters). - Behaviour: Fade when idle, mouseover, keybinds, macros, empty buttons (action bars); Fade until hovered, In combat, Combat fade (minimap, tracker); Show/visibility rules (cooldowns, resources, buff reminder, kicks); Include me (kicks); Sort, Direction, Groups as, Arrange (group frames); Health/Power text, Show name, Portrait (unit frames); Typing box position (chat); Style, Compact, Hide when empty (tracker); Hide it (Blizzard frames).

B3. Options rows that write the same position, size or scale keys as a mover

No Options page has x/y/point/offset coordinate sliders (grep across every module's Options). The only position writes outside Layout are: - ActionBars "Match Blizzard's layout / Undo / Default bar layout" (ActionBars/Options.lua:75-100 -> Bars.lua:240-320, which calls Layout:Move/Layout:Reset); - Cooldowns "Stack the bars" (Cooldowns/Options.lua:174-182 -> Layout:Stack); - Meters "Stack the windows" (Meters/Options.lua:72-80; Windows.lua:870-876); - the GroupFrames and UnitFrames "Reset positions" buttons (GroupFrames/Options.lua:126, 296, 446; UnitFrames/Options.lua:79); - QoL "Reset windows" (QoL/Options.lua:60); - the tracker maxHeight -> Layout:Move (Tracker/Window.lua:1102); - the chat resize grip (Chat/Mover.lua:106); - the GroupFrames header resize (GroupFrames/Headers.lua:320); - Meters window drag and resize (Meters/Windows.lua:417-423, 605, 638).

Size and scale duplicates. In the Gear column, "same" means the gear shows the same spec object through PickSpecs, "copy" means a second spec writing the same key, and "page only" means the row changes the moved frame's footprint but is not in the gear.

Gear only (no row on any Options page): chat width/height, actionbars.leaveSize, eyeScale, microScale, bagsBarScale, minimap.difficultyScale, and every blizzardMovers[key].scale/hide.

B4. Problems and inconsistencies found in move mode

  1. Cancel restores positions only. Size, scale and hide changes made in the gear dialog during the same session stay (Layout.lua:1144-1149 vs Dialog.lua:102-120). Edit Mode has no Cancel at all.
  2. The Minimap gear silently drops two rows because of a label mismatch (Minimap/Frame.lua:200 vs Minimap/Options.lua:449, 458). PickSpecs gives no warning (BorderSpecs.lua:60-74).
  3. Gear panels are cached per session (Dialog.lua:103-118). - The raid gear stays on the first bracket it was opened for. - Look specs that join a page only when Options loads (Tooltips "Background opacity", Tracker "Background") are missing when the gear is opened first.
  4. The battleRes gear dialog is empty on WoW Forever (its spec is in a retail-only section).
  5. Positions are one set per profile (layouts.Default), unrelated to Blizzard Edit Mode layouts, while the toolbar settings are account-wide (global.layoutTools).
  6. Mover colors use the class color, not the user's highlight/accent (Layout.lua:680-685). Guides, the toolbar and the grid use the fixed green "accent" (Layout.lua:408, Toolbar.lua:50, 115).
  7. Diagnostics left on: global.layoutLog with debugstack and global.layoutProbe are written every session (Layout.lua:287-295, 1212-1232).
  8. No Esc key leaves move mode. The nudge override bindings stay active while a mover is selected (Layout.lua:825-842).
  9. Unclear jump targets: - queueEye jumps to ActionBars "Extra buttons", which has no eye setting. - vehicleLeave and instanceDifficulty have no page jump. - Blizzard movers have neither a page nor any Options row.
  10. Two specs write party.power. The gear and the page use different spec builders that apply through different code paths (unsure whether this matters).

Part 7

Regression checklist: skinned and replaced Blizzard frames

Built from the current code on branch config-simplify (0.0.854). Run it after the settings-UI refactor, on retail with /rl, and repeat the items marked Forever on WoW Forever. Each line is something the code does today and must still do. File paths are relative to the repo root; file:line points at the settings key or the code that does the work.

How to test the common parts: - Combat: fight a training dummy and keep combat up while you try the item. "Blocked" means "Interface action failed because of an AddOn", or BugSack catching ADDON_ACTION_BLOCKED/FORBIDDEN. - Off switch: turn the setting off in /lui, /rl, and check that Blizzard's frame comes back and works. Frames hidden through ns.Hider (LixardUI/Core/Hider.lua:286) can't be un-hidden at runtime, so every "Blizzard comes back" line needs a /rl. - Module off: Options › System › Modules, or /lui disable <module> + /rl. This uses the addon enable state (LixardUI/Core/Modules.lua:101-109), not a profile key, so a profile switch never turns a module on or off. - Hider log: /dump LixardUI.Hider.log shows the last 20 hide/re-park actions (Hider.lua:238-246). Use it when a Blizzard frame shows up where it shouldn't. - Combat queue: ns.Combat:Run(key, fn) (LixardUI/Core/Combat.lua:13-30) runs at once out of combat and queues until PLAYER_REGEN_ENABLED in combat. There is no ns.AfterCombat in the code. - Watch for errors: keep BugSack (or /console scriptErrors 1) on for the whole run. Any new error fails the run, except the known Blizzard BuffFrame:213 on Forever.

Every pass

Options window (LixardUI_Options, load-on-demand via LixardUI/Core/Entry.lua:40-49): - ☐ /lui toggles the window (Entry.lua:77-83); /lui options opens it - ☐ Addon compartment click opens it, tooltip shows on hover (Entry.lua:85-98) - ☐ Game menu › Options › AddOns › LixardUI shows the stub page; "Open LixardUI settings" opens our window (Entry.lua:100-115) - ☐ Esc closes it (UISpecialFrames, LixardUI_Options/Options.lua:1478); drag moves it and the spot is remembered after /rl (Options.lua:936-950, 1154, 1477) - ☐ Chapter bar (Look, Frames, Buttons & buffs, Combat & groups, World, Social, Items & windows, System) and the view bar under it switch pages (Options.lua:37-87); the last view per chapter is remembered (navLast, Options.lua:93-100) - ☐ Search: typing filters every page (Options.lua:831-881, 1261-1269); clicking a result opens its page and sub-page and flashes the row (Options.lua:744-750); clearing the box returns to the page you were on (Options.lua:955-992) - ☐ Sub-pages and tabs: pages with sub sections get a sub-page list and, when long, a tab strip (Options.lua:133-156, 1183-1190); switching keeps the scroll and folds right - ☐ Folds: group headings open/close and stay that way after /rl (global.optionsGroups, Options.lua:90, 427, 510); "Open all folds" / "Fold all" work when there is more than one group (Options.lua:1129-1130, 1457-1460) - ☐ Advanced rows: advanced = true rows sit in a folded "Advanced" card at the end of their sub-page (Options.lua:595-636). The exception is a page with no sub-pages and two or more sections that hold advanced rows: those rows stay inline in their own sections (Options.lua:598-614). (There is no "Show advanced" switch any more, see the diff section.) - ☐ Footer "Show help text" switch shows/hides the row explanations and page description (global.showHelp, Options.lua:1329-1338, header Options.lua:700) - ☐ Reload bar appears when a row asks for a reload (ns:RequestReload, LixardUI/Core/Modules.lua:120-127) and "Reload now" reloads (Options.lua:907-910, 1538-1548) - ☐ Reset: System › Profiles › Manage › "Reset this profile" needs two clicks (LixardUI_Options/Pages/Profiles.lua:96-107, confirm LixardUI_Options/Builder.lua:24-41); every module re-applies; /lui profile reset does the same (LixardUI/Core/Commands.lua:113-115) - ☐ Profile switch: the footer profile dropdown (Options.lua:1316-1319) and /lui profile <name> both re-apply every module without errors; Copy from another profile and Delete a profile (two clicks) work (Profiles.lua:84-120); Friends settings stay the same, because they are account-wide (ns.DB.global.friends, LixardUI_Friends/Data.lua:8) - ☐ Import/Export footer links round-trip a profile (Options.lua:1321-1324); /lui export, /lui import - ☐ Look › Window material: Window texture, Scale strip, Use my class color, Highlight color and Reset highlight color repaint every open window live (ns.DB.profile.windowTexture / windowStrip, LixardUI_Options/Pages/General.lua:18-70) - ☐ Look › Borders and corners: Corners and "Border color everywhere › Apply to all" repaint every module (General.lua:222-253)

Layout and movers: - ☐ /lui move (Commands.lua:261 → LixardUI/Layout/Layout.lua:1133) and the footer "Move frames" button (Options.lua:1340-1344) open layout mode: every mover shows, drags, snaps to grid and frames, its gear dialog opens - ☐ Toolbar Save closes layout mode (LixardUI/Layout/Toolbar.lua:299); Cancel restores the snapshot (Layout.lua:1144-1149) - ☐ /lui move while Blizzard's Edit Mode is open prints "Close Blizzard's Edit Mode..." and does nothing (Layout.lua:1134-1136) - ☐ Entering combat closes layout mode and it comes back when combat ends (Layout.lua:1171-1178, 1247-1248); protected movers apply after combat (Layout.lua:208-209)

Reloads and combat: - ☐ /rl with every module on: no errors, no "couldn't start" print - ☐ /rl while in combat: no blocked action; Minimap, unit frames and group frames print "...when combat ends" and appear after combat; Hider frames get hidden after combat (Hider.lua:289-291) - ☐ Esc closes the top LixardUI window first (UISpecialFrames), then the game menu

Core: Blizzard frames moved by core (LixardUI/Layout/Blizzard.lua)

Also PlayerCastingBarFrame / Forever CastingBarFrame, taken out of UIParentBottomManagedFrameContainer in a pcall (CastBars.lua:65-71, 82-87); TotemFrame (Extras.lua:96, 185); PlayerPowerBarAlt (Extras.lua:172, 186); PersonalResourceDisplayFrame (Resources.lua:739-747, 783).

Settings: ns.DB.profile.unitframes (Data.lua:49-65), page "unitframes", Frames chapter (sub-pages Player, Target, Target of target, Focus, Pet, Boss, Arena, Extras with Frame/Texts/Auras/Cast bar tabs, Options.lua:650+). - <unit>.enabled — "Use the LixardUI %s" (Options.lua:104-107), reload - <unit>Cast.enabled — "Use the LixardUI cast bar" (Options.lua:315-318), reload - center resources hideBlizzard (hides only when the class resource bar is also on, Resources.lua:739-744) — "Hide Blizzard's personal resource display" (Options.lua:411-413), reload; class resource bar enabled (Options.lua:410) live - totems.enabled — "Totem and summon timers" (Options.lua:511); altPower.enabled — "Encounter bar" (Options.lua:517) - A profile switch that flips any enabled calls RequestReload("unitframes") (Frames.lua:801-810)

  • ☐ Player, target, ToT, focus, pet, boss 1-5, arena 1-5 show for the right units; click targets, right-click menu, tooltips
  • ☐ /rl in combat: frames print "...appear when combat ends." and build after (Frames.lua:861-864)
  • ☐ Changing size/layout in combat queues and applies after (Frames.lua:729, Options.lua:310, 505, Auras.lua:183-186)
  • ☐ Blizzard frames stay hidden after vehicle, pet battle and cutscene (keep re-parks them)
  • ☐ Cast bars: cast, channel, empower, interrupt; Blizzard's player cast bar never shows while ours is on
  • ☐ Totems (right-click dismiss), encounter bar, personal resource display hidden when asked
  • ☐ Turning each frame off asks for a reload and Blizzard's comes back after it
  • ☐ Forever: CastingBarFrame hidden; swing timer (Swing.lua:34, 250, 280); name parts row (Options.lua:757)

Party and raid (LixardUI_GroupFrames)

Blizzard frames hidden via Hider (reparent, keep): PartyFrame, CompactPartyFrame when party is on; CompactRaidFrameContainer when raid is on (LixardUI_GroupFrames/Headers.lua:405-416, 498, 566-567). The raid manager (CompactRaidFrameManager) is left to QoL.

Settings: ns.DB.profile.groupframes (Data.lua:7), page "groupframes". "Use LixardUI party frames" (Options.lua:389), "Use LixardUI raid frames" (Options.lua:464), tank/assist switches and count (Options.lua:357, 367), all reload; profile flips call RequestReload (Headers.lua:503-516).

  • ☐ Party, raid up to 40, tanks, assists show with the right members; party hides in a raid (state driver, Headers.lua:454)
  • ☐ Click targets, right-click menu, click casting; range, aggro, dispel glow, icons, heals, auras, cast bars
  • ☐ /rl in combat: headers print "...appear when combat ends." (Headers.lua:590-593)
  • ☐ Change a party/raid setting in combat: no blocked action. Apply/reorder/texture/tank updates are deferred (Headers.lua:350-379, 585, Tanks.lua:195, Buttons.lua:1266, 1294); changing the hidden-auras list in combat calls RelayoutButtons directly (Headers.lua:586)
  • ☐ /lui grouptest party|raid|raid40|off
  • ☐ Party/raid off + /rl: Blizzard's compact frames are back

Skins (LixardUI_Skins, 35 keys)

How it works: load-on-demand module "Blizzard window skins" (LixardUI/Core/Modules.lua:24). Each skin is S:Register(key, name, blizzardAddon, apply) (LixardUI_Skins/Engine.lua:96-100); skins for load-on-demand Blizzard addons run on that addon's ADDON_LOADED (Engine.lua:110-137); a failing skin is caught by pcall and shows "Couldn't be styled" on its row (Engine.lua:83-94).

Settings: - ns.DB.profile.skins.enabled[key], default on (missing = on) (Engine.lua:15, 21-28); every change calls ns:RequestReload("skins"). Row: Items & windows › Blizzard windows › Windows, one switch per skin, built from S.list (LixardUI_Options/Pages/Skins.lua:24-41, 67-71) - The character skin has a second row: System › Modules › Skins › "Character window" (LixardUI_Options/Pages/Modules.lua:43-51) - skins.portraits — Look › "Show window portraits and icons", advanced, reload (Skins.lua:47-56) - skins.glass — Look › "Smoked glass look", live repaint (Skins.lua:58-64, Engine.lua:70-79) - skins.questContrastBefore — set when gossip/quest skins force CVar questTextContrast to 4 (Windows/Quests.lua:6-14), restored at load when both are off (Quests.lua:16-23, Init.lua:3) - With the Skins module off, the page shows "Skins are turned off" (Skins.lua:10-22); the Skins page reads ns.Skins when Options loads, so Skins must still load before Options

Combat rules inside the skins: S:Backdrop only raises the frame level of unprotected frames (Engine.lua:164-166); close buttons, tabs, max/min buttons and title moves skip protected frames in combat (Widgets.lua:80, 172, 508, 623); the Forever character skin skips protected CloseButton, TitleContainer and ModeTabs (Windows/Character.lua:447, 456, 462).

For every key: open the window, use its main action, check its tabs/scroll/search/close, then turn the key off, /rl, and check Blizzard's look is back. Then turn it on again, /rl.

  • ☐ Look: "Smoked glass look" toggles live on every open skinned window; "Show window portraits and icons" brings back the portrait rings after /rl
  • ☐ gamemenu (GameMenuFrame, GameMenu.lua:11-23): Esc menu; every button, incl. buttons re-made by InitButtons
  • ☐ popups (StaticPopup1-4, Popups.lua:25-29): delete item (typed DELETE), sell refundable, quit, AH and COD confirms; Enter/Esc
  • ☐ character (CharacterFrame, PaperDoll slots, stats pane, sidebar tabs, model controls, ReputationFrame, SkillsFrame, Character.lua:435-603): slots with gear text, enchant/gems, refresh on equip; stats; right pane toggle; tabs. Open and close it in combat: no blocked action. Both the Skins page row and the Modules page "Character window" row switch it
  • ☐ Forever character: own backdrop with title/tabs lifted (Character.lua:437-466), item level block on the stats pane (Character.lua:100-117, 498), Ranged slot gear text, Forever enchant slot list (Character.lua:19, 30-32)
  • ☐ currency (TokenFrame, TokenFramePopup, CurrencyTransferMenu, CurrencyTransferLog, Character.lua:605-610), statistics (StatisticsFrame, Character.lua:612-614): lists, expand, options popup, transfer
  • ☐ spells (PlayerSpellsFrame spellbook + talents + loadout dialogs, Spells.lua:105-135): tabs, search, click to cast from the spellbook, drag a spell to a bar, page while in combat — no blocked action (DisplayViewsForCurrentPage hook, Spells.lua:120); talents apply, loadout import/create/edit dialogs; QoL talent panel's overwrite button follows the skin (LixardUI_QoL/TalentPanel.lua:669). Retail only (Blizzard_PlayerSpells)
  • ☐ bags (ContainerFrame1-13, ContainerFrameCombinedBags, BagItemSearchBox, Bags.lua:22-28): only visible with the Bags module off
  • ☐ merchant (MerchantFrame, items, buyback, tabs, filter, Merchant.lua:13-31): visible with Vendor off or "Use the LixardUI vendor window" off
  • ☐ mail (MailFrame, OpenMailFrame, send boxes, Mail.lua:25-53): visible with Mail off or its switch off

  • ☐ gossip (GossipFrame, Quests.lua:26-38), quest (QuestFrame panels, buttons moved to the bottom corners, Quests.lua:40-82): options, accept/decline/complete, rewards, scroll; dark text readable
  • ☐ worldmap (WorldMapFrame border, NavBar, overlays, QuestMapFrame log/details/side tabs, Map.lua:799-850): M open/close, maximize, zoom/pan, breadcrumbs, filters, quest log list/track/abandon/share, details back, events, legend, World Quests tab (Maps). Forever: wider backdrop (Map.lua:816-821)
  • ☐ loot (LootFrame), trade (TradeFrame), readycheck (ReadyCheckListenerFrame, buttons re-placed; a ready check during combat), dressup (DressUpFrame), itemtext (ItemTextFrame), addons (AddonList) (More.lua:23-98)
  • ☐ groupfinder (PVEFrame, GroupFinderFrame buttons, LFD/raid finder, LFGListFrame search/applications/entry creation, LFGListApplicationDialog, LFGListInviteDialog, More.lua:411-458): queue dungeons/raid finder, premade search, apply, delist
  • ☐ macros (MacroFrame, More.lua:460-472), inspect (InspectFrame slots with gear text, More.lua:529-545)

Other modules that hide Blizzard frames (not re-audited for this pass)

These were outside this audit's scope; the hide calls are listed so the refactor doesn't miss them. - ☐ Auras: BuffFrame, DebuffFrame via Hider (reparent, unregister, keep) (LixardUI_Auras/Auras.lua:297-302); right-click cancel, weapon enchants, hidden auras list (LixardUI_Auras/Options.lua:88) - ☐ Info bars: StatusTrackingBarManager, MainMenuExpBar, ReputationWatchBar, MainStatusTrackingBarContainer, SecondaryStatusTrackingBarContainer hidden always, even with all three LixardUI bars off (LixardUI_DataBars/Bars.lua:458-470); "Show the %s" rows need /rl (LixardUI_DataBars/Options.lua:32) - ☐ Damage meter: DamageMeter hidden when style is "lixard" (LixardUI_Meters/Skin.lua:19-22, LixardUI_Meters/Options.lua:24-42); "Blizzard" style skins it, "Off" leaves it - ☐ QoL: CompactRaidFrameManager hidden when "Hide Blizzard's raid tools" is on (LixardUI_QoL/GroupTools.lua:292-296, LixardUI_QoL/Options.lua:281-286), turning it back on needs /rl - ☐ Cooldowns: EssentialCooldownViewer, UtilityCooldownViewer, BuffIconCooldownViewer, BuffBarCooldownViewer arranged into holders (LixardUI_Cooldowns/Manager.lua:11-14); Edit Mode moves the holder; backup/restore refused in combat - ☐ QoL Move Blizzard windows skips windows LixardUI replaces (ns.WindowMoveSkips, LixardUI_Auction/Window.lua:267-268, LixardUI_Mail/Window.lua:173-174) and includes MerchantFrame (LixardUI_QoL/Move.lua:12)

Diff vs old checklist (docs/regression-checklist.md)

Stale: items in the old list that no longer match the code 1. Header: it targets branch design/simplify-and-identity and docs/audit.md section 3. The current branch is config-simplify. 2. Every pass › "Theme switch (General › Theme)": there is no Theme row. The look is now Look › Window material (Window texture, Scale strip, class color, Highlight color) (General.lua:18-70). 3. Every pass › "Help and Show advanced toggles": there is no "Show advanced" switch any more. global.showAdvanced and ShowAdvanced() still exist but nothing calls them (Options.lua:33, 189-191). Advanced rows now sit in folded "Advanced" cards. Help is the footer switch "Show help text". 4. Character (LixardUI_Character) section: the module is gone (removed in 0.0.568) and is not in Modules.lua:8-28. The character window is now only the character skin, and the Modules page has a "Character window" row for it. 5. Skins › "34 keys": there are 35. talents is not a key; talents are part of spells. pvp is new. 6. Skins › "character (Character module off)": that qualifier no longer applies. The skin is always the character path. 7. Skins › bags / merchant / mail / auctionhouse "(module off)": Auction, Mail and Vendor now also have their own enabled switch, so the skin shows with that switch off too. 8. Party and raid › "relayouts aren't deferred": most of them are deferred now (Headers.lua:350-379, 585). Only RelayoutButtons on the hidden-auras change is unguarded (Headers.lua:586). 9. Action bars › "re-anchors aren't guarded": partly fixed. Micro Anchor now defers for protected frames (Micro.lua:242). The eye anchor and the Layout refit hooks are still unguarded. 10. Friends › "Invite lists": the tab is now called "Lists". Keystones is retail-only. 11. Friends › Raid / Ignore / Recent Allies: unchanged, but Friends settings are account-wide (global.friends), so the profile-switch item in Every pass doesn't cover them. 12. Mail and Vendor › "Module off": both can now also be turned off with their own switch, without disabling the module.

Missing: frames or behaviour in the code that the old list doesn't cover 1. Blizzard movers: External defensives and Boss emotes, and the three separate encounter warning holders. 2. Skins: the pvp skin; Look rows "Smoked glass look" (live) and "Show window portraits and icons"; the quest text contrast CVar restore; the Forever character skin branch. 3. Bags: "Use the LixardUI bank" (replaceBank) and the "Buy tabs" Blizzard-bank-once path. There is also no switch for the bag window itself. 4. Maps: the in-world waypoint muting SuperTrackedFrame, /way without TomTom, and the retail-only World Quests section. 5. Tracker: Forever's QuestWatchFrame/WatchFrame, keep re-parking after vehicles, and queued untrack/share/abandon. 6. Action bars: Blizzard MultiBar5-7 are hidden even when bars 6-8 are off; micro and bag bar modes (style/mouseover/hide/blizzard); the stance/pet reload. 7. Unit frames: ComboFrame, the arena prep frames, PersonalResourceDisplayFrame, the cast bar leaving the managed container, and the Forever swing timer. 8. Chat: QuickJoinToastButton and ChatFrameNButtonFrame hidden, the reparented channel/menu buttons, the whispers tab, and the fact that there is no master switch. 9. Tooltips: the full list of skinned tooltip frames, QueueStatusFrame, and that there is no master switch. 10. Minimap: the build deferred in combat, HybridMinimap, the difficulty holder, rotate vs Edit Mode, and the unguarded button/drawer relayout in combat. 11. Vendor: the SetMerchantFilter side effect, and the Forever risk from unguarded C_MerchantFrame.GetItemInfo / IsMerchantItemRefundable. 12. Every pass: the reload bar, folds, footer profile dropdown/Import/Export/Move frames, the Edit Mode refusal in /lui move, the Hider log, and the fact that there is no ns.AfterCombat.
